### [34] [SOLO: Certified-Recall Metric Similarity Search with Scan-Only Sampled Inverted Lists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02387)
*Édgar Chávez*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SOLO是一种用于通用度量空间近似最近邻搜索的索引，其服务路径不含任何排序启发式，仅通过随机样本路由并对触及的倒排列表做真距评估，从而提供了可计算的召回率证书，并在内存受限下取得高召回的竞争性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有近似最近邻搜索（尤其是导航图方法）依赖排序启发式，召回率难以精确保证，且缺乏类似“召回证书”的对象；同时在高召回下对内存的占用较高。本文希望构建一个无排序启发式、召回可证且内存高效的索引。

Method: 核心是一条递归规则：对集合采样、将每个对象投递到其b个最近样本点、对超出界限的列表进行分裂，并始终扫描叶子。查询被路由到数据库随机样本的k_s个最近点，触及的每个倒排列表内对象均用真距评估。整个索引递归应用同一规则，包括用同一规则索引路由器。

Result: 召回率等于由索引可计算的覆盖概率，单次基于查询样本的真值遍历可一次性认证所有工作点。工作曲面服从等功定律recall≈f(b·k_s)。在Deep-100M上，1GB常驻内存（每对象10.7字节）达召回0.9977，256MB达0.9964；Deep-1B上512MB达0.9925（深度3时96MB）。插入等价于一次搜索，删除精确。吞吐量在具备条件的硬件上具竞争力，在两路32核服务器上10^8规模可达调优HNSW的1.8倍。

Conclusion: SOLO以无排序启发式、可计算的召回证书以及相等工作量法则，提供了在严格内存约束下仍保持高召回与竞争力吞吐的近似最近邻搜索方案，并展示了相对于HNSW、DiskANN、GRAFT、NAPP、misi和SPANN等方法的优势。

Abstract: We present SOLO, an index for approximate nearest-neighbor search in general metric spaces whose serving path contains no ranking heuristic of any kind: a query is routed to the $k_s$ nearest points of a random sample of the database, and every object in the touched posting lists is evaluated with the true distance. Because nothing must outrank anything, recall equals a coverage probability computable from the stored index: one ground-truth pass over a query sample certifies every operating point at once, without serving any of them -- a recall certificate, and for a navigable graph no analogous object exists at any price. The whole index is one recursive rule -- sample the collection, post each object to its $b$ nearest sample points, split any list that outgrows a bound, always scan the leaves -- and its operating surface obeys an equal-work law, recall $\approx f(b \cdot k_s)$, whose level is a one-scalar signature of the dataset. The same scan-only structure gives a serving floor no graph architecture reaches once the router is itself indexed by the same rule: Deep-100M served at recall 0.9977 from 1 GB of resident memory (enforced cap, 10.7 bytes per object) and at 0.9964 from 256 MB, Deep-1B at recall 0.9925 from 512 MB (and from 96 MB at depth 3), inserts that are one search, and deletes that are exact. Throughput is competitive where the hardware allows it -- up to $1.8\times$ a tuned HNSW at $10^8$ on a two-socket 32-core server, with operating points to the right of where that graph saturates -- and the tables report it against HNSW, DiskANN, GRAFT, NAPP, misi, and SPANN's assignment rule on the same hardware and ground truth.

</details>


### [35] [TREMOR: Template Matching for Large Seismic Data Collections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02534)
*Manos Chatzakis,Rodrigo Flores-Allende,Mikael Freire,Yoann Cano,Léonard Seydoux,Themis Palpanas*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出TREMOR，一个面向模板匹配的分布式数据序列处理框架，用于高效处理大规模地震波形数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地震台网持续记录大量波形数据，地震学家通过模板匹配检测地震等地球物理事件。但模板匹配处理耗时巨大，尤其是数据规模超过单机内存容量时，随着数据持续增长，这一挑战愈发严峻。

Method: 提出TREMOR——一个用于模板匹配的分布式数据序列处理框架，旨在高效处理大规模波形集合，并将其应用于两个真实世界的地震模板匹配用例。

Result: 通过大量实验评估，TREMOR比最佳竞争方法快高达12倍，同时返回相同且精确的结果。

Conclusion: TREMOR能高效应对大规模地震数据的模板匹配任务，在保证结果精确的前提下显著提升处理效率。

Abstract: Seismic station networks continuously record the ground velocity at several locations on earth in the form of waveform data series, which seismologists analyze to detect various kinds of geophysical events, including earthquakes. Some of these events are of particular interest: they are called templates and are used to search the seismic data collections for matching, similar events. This is known as template matching, and is a fundamental task in seismology, serving as the backbone for various seismic analyses. However, template matching requires extensive processing times, especially for seismic collections that exceed the memory capacity of a single machine. This poses a significant challenge to seismologists and is becoming worse as the seismological datasets continue to grow in size. In this paper, we introduce TREMOR, a distributed data series processing framework for template matching, designed to efficiently handle large waveform collections. We apply TREMOR to two representative real-world seismic use cases for template matching and, through an extensive experimental evaluation, we demonstrate its efficiency, with TREMOR being up to 12x faster than the best competing method, while returning the same, exact results.

</details>


### [36] [Look Here or Look Across: Unified Cardinality Constraints for N-ary Relationships](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02603)
*Huanyi Chen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文针对ER图中边上的基数约束存在两种相反读法所导致的歧义问题，提出了一种不偏向任一读法的记法Card(R; p; q) = (lower, upper)，可表达每条边上的多个约束，并给出了n元关系集所允许的约束数量、设计自动确定的约束、以及分解与增广两条推理规则及其成立条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ER图中边上的基数约束有两种完全相反的解读：UML和Chen原始模型按同侧实体集读取，而标准数据库教材按对侧实体集读取，二者恰好相反，读者若采用错误读法会得到与设计者本意相反的结果。二元关系的困难仅限于解释层面，但三元及以上关系更糟：三元关系集允许12个基数约束，而三条边的图最多只能承载3个。因此需要一种既能消除歧义又不偏袒任一方、且不限于每边一个约束的记法。

Method: 提出记法Card(R; p; q) = (lower, upper)以消除歧义；推导n元关系集所允许的约束数量；分析设计在不显式写出时即可确定的约束；给出分解（decomposition）与增广（augmentation）两条由一约束导出另一约束的推理规则，并说明各自成立（sound）所需的条件；最后用一个案例研究进行演示。

Result: 给出了n元关系集所允许的基数约束数量；明确了哪些约束是设计本身自动确定的（无需写出）；提出并论证了分解与增广两条推理规则及其成立条件；案例研究将三条业务需求转化为三条显式约束，并额外得到九条由设计自行决定的约束。

Conclusion: 所提记法Card(R; p; q)能在不偏袒任何一种读法的前提下消除基数约束的歧义，突破每边一个约束的限制，并配合约束计数、自动确定约束分析及分解与增广推理规则，为ER模型中的基数约束提供更可靠的表达与推理框架。

Abstract: A cardinality constraint written on an edge of an entity-relationship diagram admits two opposite readings. Under the reading used by UML and by Chen's original model, the label is read with the entity set on the same side of the relationship; under the reading used by standard database textbooks, it is read with the entity set on the opposite side. The two readings are exact opposites, so a reader who assumes the wrong one takes away the opposite of what the designer meant. For binary relationships the difficulty is confined to interpretation, because the two constraints a binary relationship set admits can both be drawn. For relationships among three or more entity sets the picture is worse: a ternary relationship set admits twelve cardinality constraints, and a diagram with three edges can carry at most three of them. This paper presents a notation, Card(R; p; q) = (lower, upper), that removes the ambiguity without taking a side in it, and that is not limited to one constraint per edge. We give the number of constraints an n-ary relationship set admits, show which of them a design determines without ever writing them down, and state two inference rules, decomposition and augmentation, that derive one constraint from another, together with the side conditions under which each is sound. We close with a worked case study that turns three business requirements into three explicit constraints and nine more that the design decides on its own.

</details>


### [37] [Query Performance Tuning with Optimal Exploration of Optimizer Cost Model Parameter Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02607)
*Wentao Wu,Xiaoying Wang,Vivek Narasayya,Surajit Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出一种确定性方法，系统探索由代价模型参数（CMP）张成的查询计划空间，并在执行时间上找到最优计划，避免随机搜索与贝叶斯优化的不稳定性，并附带减少评估开销的优化技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统代价模型参数被视为平台相关常数，部署时一次性校准后固定不变，忽略了针对具体查询进行调优的可能。近期虽有人将CMP视为可调参数，但缺乏最优性保证；而RS或BO等探索方法存在不稳定性和效果差的问题。

Method: 将CMP视为可调参数，采用确定性方式系统探索CMP张成的查询计划空间，无需穷举即可保证找到空间内所有候选计划；同时提出一组优化技术以降低候选计划执行评估的总时间。

Result: 在PostgreSQL和Microsoft SQL Server上的实验表明，调优CMP有效，能找到比RS或BO所得计划执行时间快数个数量级的查询计划。

Conclusion: 该确定性调优方法在保证最优性覆盖的同时避免了RS/BO的不稳定性，并通过评估开销优化提升了实用性，实验验证了其显著优于RS与BO。

Abstract: Modern query optimizers use analytical cost models to estimate the cost of a given query plan. Such cost models are typically functions of a set of "cost units" that specify unit CPU cost when processing a row or unit IO cost when accessing a disk page. These cost units are traditionally viewed as platform-dependent constants, that is, they require a one-shot calibration when a database is deployed on a hardware/software platform, but are fixed afterward regardless of the query being optimized for. Some very recent work has taken a different perspective by viewing these cost units as tunable parameters that we call "cost model parameters (CMPs)" in this paper. However, so far there is no approach that offers any optimality guarantee for the tuning results. We present a new approach to systematically explore the query plan space spanned by the CMPs and find the best plan in terms of execution time. Compared to alternative exploration approaches that use random search (RS) or Bayesian optimization (BO), our new approach is deterministic and, therefore, avoids the undesirable instability that is inevitable when applying RS or BO. Moreover, it is guaranteed to find all candidate plans in the query plan space without suffering from the overhead of an exhaustive enumeration. We also present a set of optimization techniques to reduce the overall evaluation time spent on executing the candidate plans found, a factor that is often overlooked by previous work but is critical from a practical point of view. Experimental evaluation on top of PostgreSQL and Microsoft SQL Server demonstrates the efficacy of tuning the CMPs, which can find query plans that are orders of magnitude faster in execution time than the ones found by RS or BO.

</details>


### [38] [RaBitQ-SSD: Split Codes and Pipelined I/O for SSD-Resident Vector Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02652)
*Yuexuan Xu,Jianyang Gao,Michael Norris,Alibek Zhakubayev,Pankaj Singh,Junjie Qi,Matthijs Douze,Cheng Long*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出 RaBitQ-SSD，通过 Split-RaBitQ 部分码驻内存与异步流水线，降低 SSD 读取并重叠 I/O 与计算，在大规模向量检索中显著超越图基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当向量集合超出 DRAM 容量时，SSD 驻留近似最近邻搜索变得关键。核心挑战在于减少 SSD 读取并隐藏 I/O 延迟。图搜索的渐进式候选发现限制了提前规划 I/O，而 IVF 搜索需完整读取倒排列表，造成不必要的 SSD 读取，二者均存在效率问题。

Method: 提出 RaBitQ-SSD，是 IVF-RaBitQ 面向 SSD 驻留向量搜索的扩展。核心为 Split-RaBitQ：在 DRAM 中保留每个二进制码的可配置前缀，使内存码存储低于每维一比特；基于该部分表示给出无偏距离估计与概率误差界。内存粗量化器确定候选列表后，该误差界支持列表内单候选剪枝，实现更细粒度 SSD 访问。同时设计异步搜索流水线，协调候选剪枝与 SSD 读取调度。

Result: 在 500 万至 10 亿向量数据集上，90% 召回率下吞吐量最高为图基基线的 1.74 倍，SSD 页读取最多减少 3.8 倍。SSD 索引比 DiskANN 小最多 7.0 倍，构建速度快 10.0 倍。在单机单 SSD 上构建并搜索 100 亿向量索引，90% 召回率下超过每秒 3000 次查询。

Conclusion: RaBitQ-SSD 通过内存前缀码、无偏距离估计与异步流水线，有效减少 SSD 读取并重叠 I/O 与计算，在大规模向量检索中兼顾吞吐、索引体积与构建效率，并成功扩展到 100 亿向量量级。

Abstract: SSD-resident approximate nearest-neighbor search is essential when vector collections exceed DRAM capacity. The challenge is to reduce SSD reads and hide I/O latency through concurrent reads and overlap with computation. However, for graph-based search, progressive candidate discovery limits advance I/O planning while IVF search reads inverted lists in full, incurring unnecessary SSD reads. In this paper, we present RaBitQ-SSD, an extension of IVF-RaBitQ for SSD-resident vector search. Specifically, we propose Split-RaBitQ, which retains a configurable prefix of each binary code in DRAM, allowing in-memory code storage below one bit per dimension. Using this partial representation, it provides an unbiased distance estimator with a probabilistic error bound. Once the in-memory coarse quantizer identifies candidate lists, this bound supports pruning individual candidates within them, enabling finer-grained SSD access. We also design an asynchronous search pipeline that coordinates candidate pruning with SSD read scheduling to reduce unnecessary reads while overlapping I/O with computation. On datasets ranging from $5$ million to $1$ billion vectors, RaBitQ-SSD delivers up to $1.74\times$ the throughput of graph-based baselines at $90\%$ recall, while reducing SSD page reads by up to $3.8\times$. Its on-SSD indexes are up to $7.0\times$ smaller and $10.0\times$ faster to build than DiskANN. We also build and search an index of $10$ billion vectors on a single machine with one SSD, achieving over $3{,}000$ queries per second at $90\%$ recall.

</details>


### [39] [Tracking State Footprints: How Agents Can Transact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03140)
*Oto Mraz,Rares Şerban,Kyriakos Psarakis,Burcu Kulahcioglu Ozkan,Asterios Katsifodimos*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该文将多智能体系统（MAS）的协调问题重新定义为数据管理问题，提出用"状态足迹"刻画智能体的读写行为，并论证MAS需要类似数据库的事务保证，但需应对新挑战与机遇，最终勾勒出下一代事务型智能体编排器与事务接口的愿景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多智能体系统越来越多地编写代码、部署基础设施、修改数据库和调用Web服务，并行执行计划时可能产生丢失更新或陈旧读取等灾难性后果。然而当前的编排器并不追踪智能体读写的状态，导致即便在简单编码任务中也会出现并发异常。因此有必要将MAS协调视为数据管理问题加以解决。

Method: 作者提出以"状态足迹"（state footprint）来描述智能体，即其在自身局部上下文与状态、编排器状态以及外部系统状态中所读取和写入的状态，并以此框架分析MAS所需的事务性保证。

Result: 分析指出MAS需要类似数据库的保证，但提供这些保证带来新挑战：与数据库事务不同，智能体不从固定模式或隔离快照中读取，且失败时无法确定性地重放；不过它们可以语义化地解决冲突而非中止，从而催生新的并发控制与冲突解决形式。

Conclusion: 论文提出面向"可事务化智能体"的愿景，规划了下一代智能体编排器，以及让外部系统参与智能体事务的事务型接口。

Abstract: Can AI agents transact? We argue that they must: as multi-agent systems (MASs) increasingly write code, deploy infrastructure, modify databases, and call web services, lost updates or stale reads can be catastrophic. Although MASs increasingly execute plans in parallel, current orchestrators do not track the state that agents read and write. As a result, concurrency anomalies manifest even in simple coding tasks. We frame MAS coordination as a data management problem and propose to describe agents by their state footprint: the state they read and write across their own local context and state, as well as the state of the orchestrator and external systems. We posit that MASs require guarantees similar to those of databases, but providing them raises new challenges and opportunities: unlike database transactions, agents do not read from a fixed schema or an isolated snapshot, and cannot be replayed deterministically upon failure. They can, however, resolve conflicts semantically instead of aborting, enabling new forms of concurrency control and conflict resolution. Towards agents that can transact, we outline a vision for next-generation agent orchestrators and transactional interfaces for external systems to participate in agentic transactions.

</details>


### [40] [TSGuard: A Real-Time Framework for Detecting and Imputing Missing Data in Streaming Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03147)
*Imane Hocine,Asma Abboura,Soror Sahri,Abhijith Senthilkumar,Yacine Hakimi,Grégoire Danoy*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出TSGuard，一个面向流式时间序列的实时演示系统，用于监控、验证与填补缺失值。它结合轻量级图感知时序填补模型与约束感知验证、回退估计和面向操作员的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式传感器应用常因故障、通信丢失或环境干扰导致观测延迟或缺失。现有填补方法虽能利用时空依赖，但多假设可离线访问未来观测，或只追求吞吐量而不保证领域合理性。

Method: TSGuard将填补嵌入更广泛的数据质量闭环：检测问题观测、填补缺失值、依据物理与空间约束验证估计值，并决定保留原值作为合理异常或在违反领域约束时替换。系统由轻量级图感知时序填补模型、约束感知验证、回退估计和解释模块组成。

Result: 以环境传感为动机场景，演示系统允许用户检查延迟传感器、比较不同填补器、定义约束，并实时验证被标记的值。

Conclusion: 其核心贡献在于结合轻量级在线时空填补、领域感知验证以及显式的保留或替换决策，同时通过交互式解释使这些决策可被操作员检查并采取行动。

Abstract: Streaming sensor applications routinely suffer from delayed or missing observations caused by faults, communication losses, or environmental interference. Although recent imputation methods exploit temporal and spatial dependencies effectively, most either assume offline access to future observations or prioritize throughput without enforcing domain plausibility. We present TSGuard, a real-time demonstration system for monitoring, validating, and imputing missing values in streaming time series. TSGuard combines a lightweight graph-aware temporal imputation model with constraint-aware validation, fallback estimation, and operator-facing explanations.
  Rather than treating imputation as an isolated prediction task, TSGuard integrates it into a broader data-quality loop: detect problematic observations, impute missing values, validate estimated against physical and spatial constraints, and either retain the original value as a plausible anomaly or replace it when it violates domain constraints. Using environmental sensing as a motivating setting, the demo enables users to inspect delayed sensors, compare imputers, define constraints, and validate flagged values in real time. The combination of lightweight online spatiotemporal imputation, domain-aware validation, and explicit retain-or-replace decisions is our central contribution, while interactive explanations make these decisions inspectable and actionable. for operators.

</details>

### [41] [Hybrid Machine Learning-Assisted Raman Spectroscopy with Generative Feature Augmentation for Pharmaceutical Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02224)
*Quach Thi Thai Binh,Ton Nu Quynh Trang,Thang B. Phan,Vu Thi Hanh Thu,Nguyen Tuan Hung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HyMLRaman混合拉曼光谱框架，结合深度特征提取、生成模型与经典机器学习分类器，用于识别六种药物残留，最高实现96.31%准确率与96.36%宏F1，并验证了低数据条件下的增强效果及交互式分析可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物残留的快速可靠识别对保障公共健康、食品安全以及实现基于拉曼光谱的实际筛查具有重要意义，而单一CNN基线在特征表达与数据有限条件下仍有提升空间。

Method: 将拉曼光谱转为光谱图像，用多种深度骨干网络编码，其中EfficientNet-B3效果最佳（1536维嵌入）；再用SVM、KNN、逻辑回归、随机森林、XGBoost和ANN等分类器，采用分层10折交叉验证训练；并在PCA降维的EfficientNet-B3潜在空间中引入基于DDPM的生成式特征增强以应对低数据问题。

Result: EfficientNet-B3与SVM的混合配置表现最佳，达到96.31%准确率和96.36%宏F1，优于独立CNN基线；DDPM增强在低数据消融中对KNN在较少训练比例下有选择性收益，且效果依赖于分类器。

Conclusion: HyMLRaman为快速、可解释的拉曼药物筛查提供了一条实用路径，并通过应用级拉曼药物分析器验证了将模型嵌入交互式分析流程的可行性。

Abstract: Rapid and reliable identification of pharmaceutical residues is important for safeguarding public health, ensuring food safety, and enabling practical Raman-based screening. In this study, we propose HyMLRaman, a hybrid Raman spectroscopy framework that combines deep spectral feature extraction, generative models, and classical machine-learning classifiers to identify six pharmaceutical compounds, including amoxicillin, chloramphenicol, ciprofloxacin, tetracycline, ibuprofen, and paracetamol. Raman spectra are converted into spectral images and encoded with several deep neural-network backbones, among which EfficientNet-B3 yields the most effective representation. The resulting 1536-dimensional embeddings are then used to train downstream classifiers, including SVM, KNN, logistic regression, random forest, XGBoost, and ANN, using stratified 10-fold cross-validation. The hybrid EfficientNet-B3--SVM configuration achieves the strongest baseline performance, reaching 96.31% accuracy and a macro-F1 score of 96.36%, outperforming the standalone CNN baseline. To address limited-data conditions, a generative model, a DDPM-based feature augmentation, is introduced in a PCA-reduced EfficientNet-B3 latent space. The low-data ablation results show that DDPM augmentation provides selective benefits, particularly for KNN with reduced training fractions, and that its effect remains classifier-dependent. Finally, an application-level Raman Pharmaceutical Analyzer demonstrates the feasibility of embedding the trained model into an interactive Raman analysis workflow. These results suggest that HyMLRaman provides a practical and interpretable route for rapid Raman-based pharmaceutical screening.

</details>


### [42] [The Price of Greenwashing: Algorithmic Verification and Market Discipline using Conformal Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02225)
*Sourav Bose,Taoufik Bouraoui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出融合SEC财务基本面与EPA设施级温室气体登记数据，用梯度提升与Mondrian保形预测构建物理排放基准，并据此定义"保形加权连续背离"(CWCD)指标来量化企业自报排放数据的偏差。计量结果显示，算法识别的排放背离与后续Tobin's Q和ROA显著负相关，证明机构资本会将环境数据造假定价为基本面管理不善的先行指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业可持续性要求不断扩大，但金融体系高度依赖企业自报排放数据，导致"漂绿"风险普遍存在。现有文献多依赖主观ESG评级或文本情绪分析，缺乏客观量化物理气候现实的经济计量手段，形成关键研究空白。为化解这一信息不对称，需要建立可数学保证的物理排放基准。

Method: 将美国SEC财务基本面数据与EPA设施级温室气体登记数据进行融合，构建企业物理排放的算法基准；采用梯度提升(gradient boosting)架构并结合Mondrian保形预测，将自报数据与算法基准之间的缺口量化为CWCD指标；再用横截面领先-滞后(lead-lag)计量设计检验该背离与市场结果的关系。

Result: 算法识别的排放背离与后续市场估值(Tobin's Q)及运营盈利能力(ROA)之间存在显著且稳健的负向关系，且统计上高度显著。该结果为反驳"市场盲目假说"提供了明确证据。

Conclusion: 研究证明机构资本会主动为环境欺骗定价，不仅视为伦理失误，更视为公司基本面管理不善的领先指标。研究结论为资产管理者和监管者大规模部署算法审计基础设施提供了定量依据。

Abstract: While corporate sustainability mandates are expanding, the systemic reliance on self-reported emissions data exposes financial markets to pervasive greenwashing. Current literature relies heavily on subjective ESG ratings or textual sentiment analysis, leaving a critical econometric gap in objectively quantifying physical climate realities. To resolve this information asymmetry, we fuse U.S. SEC financial fundamentals with facility-level EPA greenhouse gas registries to establish a mathematically guaranteed baseline of physical corporate emissions. Leveraging a gradient boosting architecture and Mondrian Conformal Prediction, we quantify the shortfall between self-reported data and this algorithmic baseline into a novel Conformal-Weighted Continuous Divergence (CWCD) metric. Evaluating this divergence via a cross-sectional lead-lag econometric design, we uncover a robust mechanism of market discipline: algorithmic emissions divergence exhibits a severe, statistically significant negative relationship with subsequent market valuation (Tobin's Q) and operational profitability (ROA). Providing definitive evidence against the market blindness hypothesis, this study proves that institutional capital actively prices environmental deception not merely as an ethical lapse, but as a leading indicator of fundamental corporate mismanagement. Ultimately, these findings provide the quantitative justification necessary for asset managers and regulators to deploy algorithmic auditing infrastructure at scale.

</details>


### [43] [State-Space Unlearning for Non-Stationary Bias in Land Surface Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02248)
*Anidipta Pal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SSU-LSF，首个面向地理科学中基于 Mamba 的状态空间模型（SSM）的机器遗忘框架，用于消除陆表预报模型状态转移矩阵中被吸收的非平稳混淆事件影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运行中的陆表预报系统（基于 Mamba 家族 SSM）会将未记录的灌溉激增、大坝调度变化、传感器重校准等非平稳混淆事件吸收进状态转移矩阵，在物理成因结束后仍长期隐性偏置 NDVI、LST 与作物物候预测。现有方法难以定位并移除这类被"记忆"在模型参数中的时空混淆。

Method: 提出 EKFac 影响函数，通过闭式矩阵指数梯度专门适配 Mamba 状态矩阵；用谱半径加权的拐点阈值法定位时间混淆足迹 Φ；在 KL 散度信赖域内执行无 Hessian 的投影梯度上升，并加入空间全变分（TV）正则化。理论上由命题 1 给出残余混淆的收敛上界。

Result: 在三个异构基准与十一个基线上，混淆削减率分别达 0.773（CropHarvest）、0.821（NDVI-LST）、0.859（ERA5）；ERA5 上最差干净域 RMSE 退化仅 4.2%；3–5 个 epoch 收敛，每次遗忘请求的 GPU 成本比全量重训低 8.4 倍。命题 1 证明残余混淆受 O((1-ρ(Ā)^Tc)/((1-ρ(Ā))μ)) 约束，且随窗口长度 Tc 增大而增长。

Conclusion: SSU-LSF 验证了面向 Mamba SSM 的机器遗忘在陆表预报中的可行性与高效性，兼顾高混淆削减率、低干净域性能损失与显著降低的计算成本，为地理科学模型的动态纠偏提供了新范式。

Abstract: Operational land surface forecasting systems built on Mamba-family Structured State Space Models absorb non-stationary confounding events (unrecorded irrigation booms, dam-operation shifts, sensor recalibrations) into their state-transition matrices, silently biasing NDVI, LST, and crop phenology predictions long after the physical cause ends. This paper introduces SSU-LSF (State-Space Unlearning for Land Surface Forecasting), the first machine-unlearning framework purpose-built for geoscientific Mamba-based SSMs. We develop EKFac influence functions specialized to the Mamba state matrices via a closed-form matrix-exponential gradient, use spectral-radius-weighted elbow thresholding to localize a temporal confounding footprint $Φ$, and apply Hessian-free projected gradient ascent within a KL-divergence trust region augmented by spatial total-variation (TV) regularization. Proposition 1 establishes that residual confounding is bounded by $\mathcal{O}\big((1-ρ(\bar{A})^{T_c})/((1-ρ(\bar{A}))μ)\big)$, which grows with the window length $T_c$. Across three heterogeneous benchmarks and eleven baselines, SSU-LSF achieves confounding reduction rates of $0.773$ (CropHarvest), $0.821$ (NDVI-LST), and $0.859$ (ERA5), with worst-case clean-domain RMSE degradation of $4.2\%$ on ERA5, converging in 3--5 epochs at $8.4\times$ lower GPU-cost per unlearning request than full retraining. Code: https://github.com/Anidipta/SSU-LSF

</details>


### [44] [Nearest-neighbour baselines for fingerprint prediction from MS/MS spectra under different assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02249)
*Ling Min Serena Khoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该报告系统比较了多种最近邻检索变体，揭示不同推理假设对MS/MS谱分子指纹预测性能的影响，旨在建立更严格的基线以促进更严谨的基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明最近邻检索在MS/MS谱分子指纹预测中提供了强基线，部分变体甚至匹配或超越深度学习模型。然而"最近邻"涵盖了一族在推理时假设信息不同的检索方法，这些差异尚未被系统研究，可能影响基准比较的公平性。

Method: 系统性地比较多种最近邻检索变体，分析它们在推理阶段所依赖的不同信息假设，并评估这些假设如何影响预测性能。

Result: 不同最近邻变体因推理假设的差异而表现出不同的性能，这些差异会显著影响与深度学习模型的对比结果。

Conclusion: 应建立更严格的基线，以实现更严谨的基准测试并更准确地衡量该领域的进展。

Abstract: It has recently been shown that nearest-neighbour retrieval provides a strong baseline for molecular fingerprint prediction from MS/MS spectra, with several variants matching or outperforming current deep learning models (Khoo and Barzilay, 2026; Liu et al., 2026; Gupta et al., 2026). Importantly, "nearest neighbour" encompasses a family of retrieval methods that differ in the information assumed to be available at inference. In this report, we systematically compare several nearest-neighbour variants and show how these differing assumptions affect performance. Our goal is to establish stricter baselines that enable more rigorous benchmarking and better measure progress in this area.

</details>


### [45] [A Manifold-Aware Topic Modeling Approach via Rank-Based Prototypes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29630)
*Thiago César Castilho Almeida,Daniel Carlos Guimarães Pedronette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 MARETopic，一种无需训练的框架，将主题发现视为基于排序的原型选择，从真实语料中挑选 K 个示例文档，性能优于神经与聚类式主题模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主题模型虽引入预训练嵌入，但神经架构产生的潜在表示缺乏对具体文本的依托；基于聚类的流程仅在事后指定代表文档，且依赖绝对距离，易受高维空间的 hubness 与各向异性扭曲。

Method: MARETopic 将嵌入投影到低维流形后构建编码序数邻域结构的排序列表，用贪心算法选取恰好 K 个真实语料示例文档，使其邻域覆盖整个语料。包含两个变体：MARETopic_Corr 用查询性能预测器与排序相关性度量打分；MARETopic_Diff 用基于排序的扩散矩阵打分，无需该度量。另引入主题间最大边际相关性（MMR）步骤提升词汇多样性。

Result: MARETopic_Corr 在类别最多的两个基准上于 Purity 与 NMI 上领先神经与聚类式主题模型；MARETopic_Diff 运行速度快 1.7 至 1.9 倍。在三次数据集中的两次上，MARETopic 无需任何梯度更新即取得最佳主题连贯性。主题间 MMR 步骤以很小的连贯性代价提升词汇多样性。

Conclusion: MARETopic 以无需训练、基于排序的原型选择方式实现主题发现，在聚类质量、运行效率与主题连贯性上具备竞争力，并提供开源代码。

Abstract: Recent topic models leverage pretrained embeddings, but neural architectures produce latent representations without grounding in specific texts, and clustering-based pipelines assign representative documents only post hoc, relying on absolute distances distorted by hubness and anisotropy in high-dimensional spaces. We introduce MARETopic, a training-free framework that casts topic discovery as rank-based prototype selection. After projecting embeddings onto a low-dimensional manifold, MARETopic builds ranked lists encoding ordinal neighborhood structure. A greedy algorithm selects exactly K exemplar documents, real corpus texts, whose neighborhoods cover the corpus. Two variants share this criterion. MARETopic$_\text{Corr}$ scores candidates with a query performance predictor and a rank correlation measure, leading Purity and NMI on the two benchmarks with the most categories, ahead of both neural and clustering-based topic models. MARETopic$_\text{Diff}$ scores them with a rank-based diffusion matrix, needs neither measure, and runs 1.7 to 1.9 times faster. Without a single gradient update, MARETopic leads topic coherence on two of three datasets. A novel inter-topic Maximal Marginal Relevance step raises vocabulary diversity at little cost in coherence. Our code is available at https://github.com/thcastilho/maretopic.

</details>


### [46] [Rank-Aware Speculative Sampling for Diffusion Draft Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02251)
*Marcello Bullo,Yanxiao Liu,Öykü Sıla Güner,Arpan Mukherjee,Deniz Gündüz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Rank-Aware Speculative Sampling (RASS)，一种基于秩感知列表耦合的扩散模型投机采样验证规则，通过按提议-目标均值位移排序候选并优化秩权重以最小化分布差异，从而在多个任务上优于 D-GRS，CIFAR-10 上约提升 20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散生成采样成本高，投机采样可通过并行验证廉价草稿状态来加速。近期树式方法（如 D-GRS）比单链草稿更有效，但 D-GRS 虽然按生成顺序逐一测试条件独立候选，却未利用候选本身蕴含的有信息量排序，浪费了可挖掘的验证潜力。

Method: RASS 针对投机草稿树设计秩感知列表耦合验证规则：将草稿候选沿提议-目标均值位移排序，采样一个秩，其权重经过优化以最小化所选提议分布与目标分布之间的总变差；随后将所选候选与目标做最大耦合，并通过残差修正保证任意秩权重下均为精确采样。

Result: 在高斯混合目标、FFHQ 无条件像素空间生成、CIFAR-10 条件生成，以及使用 COCO2014 提示的 Stable Diffusion 3.5 潜扩散上评估。以标准采样与投机采样的目标模型评估次数之比衡量，RASS 在各设置下均优于 D-GRS，在匹配计算预算下 CIFAR-10 增益约达 20%。

Conclusion: RASS 利用候选的秩信息进行秩感知列表耦合验证，在保持精确采样（目标律）的同时提升验证效率，是一种在多种扩散生成任务上普遍优于 D-GRS 的投机采样验证方法。

Abstract: Speculative sampling accelerates diffusion generation by verifying inexpensive draft states in parallel while preserving the target law. Recent tree-based methods allocate the parallel compute budget more effectively than single-chain drafts, as demonstrated by Diffusion Greedy Rejection Sampling (D-GRS). D-GRS generates $K$ conditionally independent candidates per node, and sequentially tests them in their generation order. Yet the sampled candidates admit an informative ranking without additional target-model evaluations. To exploit this, we introduce Rank-Aware Speculative Sampling (RASS), a verification rule for speculative draft trees based on rank-aware list coupling. RASS orders draft candidates along the proposal-target mean displacement and samples a rank with weights optimized to minimize total variation between the selected-proposal and target laws. Finally, the selected candidate is maximally coupled with the target, with residual correction ensuring exact sampling for any choice of rank weights. We evaluate RASS on a Gaussian-mixture target, unconditional pixel-space generation on FFHQ, conditional generation on CIFAR-10, and latent diffusion with Stable Diffusion 3.5 using COCO2014 prompts. Measured by the ratio of standard to speculative sampling's target-model evaluation counts, RASS improves on D-GRS across the evaluated settings, with gains reaching approximately 20% on CIFAR-10 at matched compute budgets.

</details>


### [47] [Counterfactual Predictions in Scientific Emulators Without Controlled Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02252)
*Dingling Yao,Kahaan Gandhi,Valentin Duruisseaux,Boris Bonev,Francesco Locatello,Anima Anandkumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReRoute：无需受控干预数据，结合事实数据与部分机制知识，实现科学反事实/What-if预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学问题常需回答未观测的反事实（不同条件/干预/历史下会怎样）。现有模型在相关输入被独立变动时易失效；常用补救是加入受控模拟数据，但需模拟器、计算昂贵且继承模拟器假设。

Method: ReRoute将预训练主干网络中被查询的输入固定为参考值，通过已知机制通路重新引入其变化，并在原始事实数据上微调，将下游效应留给已学动力学；在明确结构假设下给出因果可识别性结果，核心论证用Lean机器验证。

Result: 在可控平流-扩散系统中实现高精度反事实预测；在SOTA气候模拟中，CO2严重分布偏移下聚合气候误差降低18.2-31.8%，标准条件下保持性能，成本远低于额外受控模拟重训练；在ERA5历史再分析训练的模拟器上，固定CO2反事实下保留更多由观测边界条件暗示的地表增暖。

Conclusion: ReRoute无需受控干预数据即可进行针对性反事实预测，兼顾准确性与低成本，并可推广至气候模拟等科学场景。

Abstract: Many scientific questions require reasoning about what was never observed: What if the conditions, interventions, or history had been different? Models can predict accurately on observed data yet fail on such what-if queries when correlated inputs are varied independently. A common remedy is to add controlled simulation data in which these factors are explicitly disentangled, but this requires access to a simulator, can be computationally expensive, and inherits the simulator's modeling assumptions. We introduce ReRoute, a framework for targeted scientific what-if prediction that combines factual data with partial mechanistic knowledge, without requiring controlled intervention data for adaptation. ReRoute fixes the queried input of a pretrained backbone to a reference value, reintroduces its variation through a known mechanistic pathway, and fine-tunes on the original factual data, while leaving downstream effects to the learned dynamics. We provide a causal identification result for this construction under explicit structural assumptions, with the core argument machine-checked in Lean. After showing that ReRoute achieves highly accurate counterfactual predictions in a controlled advection-diffusion system where exact responses are available, we turn to state-of-the-art climate emulation. On held-out coupled-climate interventions, ReRoute reduces aggregate climate error by 18.2-31.8% under severe CO$_2$ distribution shifts while preserving skill under standard conditions, at a small fraction of the cost of retraining on additional controlled simulations, without even accounting for the substantial expense of generating such data. Finally, on an emulator trained from historical ERA5 reanalysis, where no counterfactual reference exists, ReRoute preserves substantially more of the surface warming implied by the observed boundary conditions under a fixed-CO$_2$ counterfactual.

</details>


### [48] [Approximation Property of Dropout Neural Networks: Sobolev Rates and Confidence Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02253)
*Jia-He Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究随机 dropout ReLU 网络对 Sobolev 空间单位球的均匀逼近，构造常数深度网络并给出与下界相匹配的误差-规模刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用逼近性质无法刻画随机实现在给定精度下所需的网络规模，需要在 dropout 随机保留机制下单次采样网络的概率化逼近保证。

Method: 对 W^{n,∞}([0,1]^d) 单位球，构造常数深度、边以概率 p 独立保留的 ReLU 网络；结合局部有界子网络、成功逼近事件上的局部化与多尺度 Taylor 分解；并用 Sobolev 容量及固定仿射函数逼近导出下界。

Result: 上界规模为 Õ_{n,d}(p^{-9}ε^{-max{d/n,2}} log(1/δ))；下界包括存活边数受容量约束，以及输出层成本量级 ((1-p)/p)ε^{-2}log(1/δ)；固定 p 与 δ<min{1/2,1-p} 时，在固定或对数深度预算下精度指数匹配，d≤2n 时置信度也匹配至对数因子。

Conclusion: 在固定或对数深度下实现 dropout ReLU 网络的精度最优刻画，并将下界推广到 W^{n,r} 目标的 L^s 误差；最优保留依赖与对数因子仍待解决。

Abstract: The universal approximation property of dropout neural networks does not by itself describe the network size required for an accurate random realization. In this work, we study approximation of the unit ball of $W^{n,\infty}([0,1]^d)$ by ReLU networks whose edges are retained independently with probability $p$. The approximation error is measured uniformly over the input domain, and the guarantee holds with probability at least $1-δ$ for a single sampled network. We construct networks of constant depth and size $\widetilde O_{n,d}(p^{-9}\varepsilon^{-\max\{d/n,2\}} \log(1/δ))$. The construction combines bounded local subnetworks, localization on a successful approximation event, and a multiscale Taylor decomposition. Conversely, Sobolev capacity imposes a lower bound on the number of surviving edges, while approximation of a fixed affine function requires an output-layer cost of order $((1-p)/p)\varepsilon^{-2}\log(1/δ)$ at sufficiently high confidence. For fixed $p\in(0,1)$ and $δ<\min\{1/2,1-p\}$, the upper and lower bounds match in the accuracy exponent under a fixed or logarithmic depth budget. When $d\leq2n$, they also match in confidence up to logarithms of accuracy. We extend the lower bounds to $W^{n,r}$ targets with $L^s$ error, and distinguish this extension from the upper bound for $W^{n,\infty}$. The optimal retention dependence and logarithmic factors remain open.

</details>


### [49] [Overcoming Challenges of Interpretive Structural Modeling with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02254)
*Everett Rush,David J. Icove,Ari Kim,Byung H. Park,Michael A. Langston*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索将大语言模型（LLM）与解释结构模型（ISM）结合，用LLM作为"不完美专家"自动完成因果图发现，以克服传统ISM依赖专家反复交互、难以扩展到大变量规模的局限。研究比较了成对、k-wise、行式与全图四种发现方法，结果表明行式（SHD=160，F1=0.77）与全图（SHD=135，F1=0.73）方法表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ISM需与领域专家反复交互直至达成共识，过程繁琐、耗费人力，且严重限制其扩展到含数百个变量的研究。作者希望借助LLM作为不完美专家实现因果图自动发现，从而提升ISM的可扩展性。

Method: 借鉴基于LLM的大规模因果图发现的研究，提出并评估一个集成的LLM-ISM方法。具体设计并对比较了四种图发现策略：成对（pairwise）、k-wise、行式（rowwise）和全图（full graph）发现方法。

Result: 在因果图发现效果上，行式方法表现最佳（SHD=160，F1-score=0.77），全图方法次之（SHD=135，F1-score=0.73）。

Conclusion: LLM可有效替代或辅助专家完成ISM建模中的因果图发现，其中行式与全图方法最具潜力，为实现可扩展的大规模ISM建模提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Interpretive Structural Modeling (ISM) is a well-known process for multi-criteria decision making. The success of ISM over other methodologies is its ability to model causal relationships, the binary scale of factors, and resulting hierarchical representation. Traditionally, the modeling process is performed by repeated interactions with subject matter experts until consensus is reached. This process is tedious, labor-intense, and most importantly limits the ability of ISM to scale to studies with hundreds of variables. Drawing on existing work of causal graph discovery with large language models (LLM) as imperfect experts, this work explores an integrated LLM-ISM approach for ISM. Pairwise, k-wise, rowwise, and full graph discovery methodologies are compared and evaluated. It is shown that causal graph discovery methods for ISM perform best using rowwise (SHD=160, F1-score=0.77) and full graph methods (SHD=135, F1-score=0.73).

</details>


### [50] [MACTS-EM: Multi-Agent Collaborative Time Series Forecasting with Emergent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02255)
*Ahmad Shahi,Mamehgol Yousefi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多智能体协作时间序列预测框架 MACTS-EM，通过专业化智能体协作、元认知调度、涌现记忆、多模态上下文整合与对抗鲁棒性，在多个领域超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测在诸多领域仍是关键挑战。现有方法在机制转换、跨域知识迁移和多模态数据整合等复杂现象上表现不足，亟需超越传统架构的新思路。

Method: 提出 MACTS-EM 框架，整合五个组件：(1) 领域专业化预测智能体，分别负责模式识别、异常检测、因果推断与不确定性量化；(2) 元认知层，用于动态智能体分配；(3) 涌现记忆机制，支持跨域模式迁移；(4) 多模态上下文整合；(5) 对抗鲁棒性组件。

Result: 在金融市场、气候模式、能源消费和疫情传播四类场景评估中，MACTS-EM 在多数场景优于现有方法：预测精度提升 8-12%，零样本迁移能力提升 22-27%，机制转换期间韧性提升 16-21%，分布偏移后恢复速度快 15-18%。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，协作式、智能体化的时间序列预测方法代表了超越传统架构的有前景方向，尤其适用于需要多分辨率时间理解与上下文适应的复杂现实场景。

Abstract: Time series forecasting remains a critical challenge across numerous domains. Despite significant advancements, existing approaches struggle with complex phenomena such as regime shifts, cross-domain knowledge transfer, and multimodal data integration. This paper introduces Multi-Agent Collaborative Time Series Forecasting with Emergent Memory (MACTS-EM), a novel framework where specialised agents collaborate to achieve superior forecasting performance. The MACTS-EM architecture integrates: (1) domain-specialised forecasting agents for pattern recognition, anomaly detection, causal inference, and uncertainty quantification; (2) a meta-cognitive layer for dynamic agent allocation; (3) an emergent memory mechanism enabling cross-domain pattern transfer; (4) multimodal contextual integration; and (5) adversarial robustness components. Evaluation across financial markets, climate patterns, energy consumption, and pandemic propagation demonstrates that MACTS-EM outperforms existing approaches in most scenarios, with 8-12% improvement in forecasting accuracy, 22-27% better zero-shot transfer capability, 16-21% enhanced resilience during regime shifts, and 15-18% faster recovery after distribution shifts. Our findings suggest that collaborative, agentic approaches to time series forecasting represent a promising direction beyond traditional architectures, particularly for complex real-world scenarios requiring multi-resolution temporal understanding and contextual adaptation.

</details>


### [51] [TRACE: A Reproducible Benchmark for Electricity Price Forecasting with Official Operational Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02256)
*Xinyi Yi,Moy Yuan,Ioannis Lestas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TRACE基准（7,300个区域-日样本，覆盖美国某主要市场五个区域），将预测截止时点可得的官方运行文本与电价配对，防止信息泄漏，并评估文本的语义对齐与预测价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电价预测（EPF）基准主要聚焦数值输入，预测时点可用文本上下文的价值未被充分评估。需构建可复现基准，系统检验文本对电价预测的作用。

Method: 构建TRACE：7,300个区域-日实例，覆盖美国某主要市场五个区域；在各预测截止时点重建官方运行文本，避免截止后信息泄漏；从语义对齐与预测价值两方面评估，并进行跨日文本错配消融实验。

Result: 语义评估与真实价格的中心变动及两侧尾部风险一致，以上尾价格风险最为稳定；在时间序列基础模型上，上尾分位数损失中位数降低7.4%；跨日文本错配消融使增益反转，低于无文本基线。

Conclusion: TRACE表明预测时点文本上下文对电价预测（尤其上尾风险）具有实际价值，且信息泄漏控制与消融验证了增益来源的可靠性。

Abstract: Electricity price forecasting (EPF) supports scheduling, bidding, and risk management in electricity markets, yet existing benchmarks focus mainly on numerical inputs, leaving the forecasting value of forecast-time textual context insufficiently evaluated. We introduce TRACE, a reproducible benchmark of 7,300 zone--day instances pairing prices from five zones in a major U.S. market with official operational text available at the forecast cutoff. TRACE reconstructs official operational text at each cutoff, preventing post-cutoff information leakage. We evaluate TRACE for semantic alignment and forecasting value. Semantic assessments align with central movement and both tail risks in ground-truth prices, most consistently for upper-tail price risk. Forecasting value is reflected in a median 7.4\% reduction in upper-tail pinball loss across time-series foundation models. A controlled cross-day text-mismatch ablation reverses the gains, falling below the no-text baseline.

</details>


### [52] [Parameter-Free Interval-Dynamic Regret under Heavy-Tailed Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02258)
*Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究每轮仅有一个无偏随机次梯度的在线凸优化问题，在未知有限条件$p$阶噪声矩（$1<p\le2$）下，提出一种无需知道$G,\sigma,p,I,P_I$的自适应学习器，实现区间自适应的遗憾界，同时保持均值梯度与噪声的独立指数，并给出对数项噪声幂的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线凸优化中随机次梯度的噪声矩与梯度尺度通常未知，且遗憾界往往需要针对不同时间区间自适应。现有方法难以在不预先知道梯度上界$G$、噪声参数$\sigma,p$及区间信息$I,P_I$的情况下，同时兼顾均值梯度项与噪声项的最优指数，并保持对任意区间的自适应能力。

Method: 提出一个自适应学习器，不依赖$G,\sigma,p,I,P_I$。分析通过控制期望校准以及限制观测尺度变化的代价，得到一般性定理，并将其与基于非均匀先验的相对熵专家分布预测进行比较。采用通用先验偏好长窗口与长重启长度，使区间代价变为$1+\log(T/n)$。

Result: 对任意长度$n$的固定区间$I$与比较器路径（$Λ_I=1+P_I/D$），学习器实现期望遗憾上界为$\min(GDn, C[GD\sqrt{n(Λ_I+\log^2(2T))} + \sigma D n^{1/p}(Λ_I+\log^2(2T))^{(p-1)/p}])$，常数为通用常数。区间自适应仅增加比较器复杂度，同时保留均值梯度与噪声的独立指数；区间代价为$1+\log(T/n)$，包含最优全时域静态速率。

Conclusion: 通过变化测度下界，在显式条件下识别出保留全时域最优保证的学习器中该对数项的噪声幂。静态比较与确定性划分可由相同决策推出，表明所提自适应方法在未知噪声参数下同时实现区间自适应与最优速率结构。

Abstract: We study online convex optimization with one unbiased stochastic subgradient per round and an unknown finite conditional $p$th noise moment, $1<p\le2$. For every fixed interval $I$ of length $n$ and comparator path with $Λ_I=1+P_I/D$, one learner achieves
  \[ E[Regret_I(u)]\le\min(GDn, C[GD\sqrt{n(Λ_I+\log^2(2T))} +σDn^{1/p}(Λ_I+\log^2(2T))^{(p-1)/p}]). \]
  The learner uses none of $G,σ,p,I,P_I$, and the constant is universal. Interval adaptation adds to comparator complexity, preserving the distinct mean-gradient and noise exponents. The analysis controls calibration in expectation and limits the cost of observation-scale changes. Its general theorem compares to distributions over predictably available experts with relative-entropy dependence on a nonuniform prior. A common prior favors long windows and long restart lengths. With the statistics supplied, the interval cost becomes $1+\log(T/n)$, including the optimal full-horizon static rate. A change-of-measure lower bound identifies the noise power of this logarithm for learners retaining a full-horizon optimal guarantee, under explicit conditions. Static comparisons and deterministic partitions follow from the same decisions.

</details>


### [53] [From Mathematical to Executable Certificates for Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02268)
*Ziyu Zhao,Xinyu Wang,Xiaowen Chang,Yixuan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出ExecCert发布时认证层，通过闭合原生证书或RRV验证候选制品对当前保留集重训练的保真度，并给出增量实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学认证与部署中有限精度制品之间存在差距，需在发布时对实际执行的候选制品进行认证，尤其在顺序删除场景下精确参考与数值状态分别演化使验证困难。

Method: ExecCert在发布时要么闭合方法原生证书，要么应用Retraining-Reference Release Verification (RRV)认证其对当前保留集重训练的保真度；针对冻结表示加可变ridge头，提出RRV的增量实现以跨删除请求维护认证证据。

Result: 在四个已发表的unlearning实现上，ExecCert保留有效证书、改变发布决策、收紧保守界并识别具体输出支持的参考保真度；顺序服务实验中RRV消除存储方程验证导致的误发布，紧贴实现误差，且增量认证在发布检查足够频繁后比新建与维护验证因子方案更便宜。

Conclusion: ExecCert为机器unlearning提供了兼顾数学保证与实际部署的发布认证方案，RRV与增量认证在实践中有效且经济。

Abstract: Machine unlearning is needed when data must be removed because of deletion requests, outdated records, or data-quality concerns, while retraining from scratch can be costly. Certified machine unlearning methods provide mathematical guarantees, while deployed systems release concrete finite-precision artifacts produced by software. To bridge the gap between mathematical guarantees and practical deployment, we introduce Executable Release Certification (ExecCert), a release-time layer that certifies the candidate artifact considered for release. ExecCert either closes a method's native certificate for the executed candidate or applies Retraining-Reference Release Verification (RRV) to certify fidelity to current retain-set retraining. Sequential deletion makes the latter nontrivial because the exact retain-set reference and the stored numerical state evolve separately. For frozen representations with a mutable ridge head, we develop an incremental realization of RRV that maintains certified evidence across deletion requests rather than reconstructing it at each release. On four published unlearning implementations, ExecCert preserves valid certificates, changes release decisions, tightens conservative bounds, and identifies the retraining-reference fidelity supported by concrete outputs. In sequential-service experiments, RRV eliminates false releases caused by stored-equation verification while closely tracking realized error, and incremental certification remains cheaper than both fresh and maintained verified-factor alternatives once release checks become sufficiently frequent.

</details>


### [54] [CLEAN: Psychometrically Consistent Incremental Cognitive Diagnosis under Concept-Space Expansion via Architectural Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02278)
*Tao He,Jinxing Xiang,Fan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CLEAN 框架，通过架构隔离实现可扩展概念空间下的增量认知诊断，保证历史诊断的逐点不变性（零表示漂移）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有增量认知诊断模型假设概念空间固定，新题带来的梯度会覆盖历史通路，造成灾难性遗忘；且仅依赖软约束保持历史诊断，难以满足心理测量一致性所要求的诊断不变性。

Method: CLEAN 包含三部分：(1) 严格拓扑二分协议，冻结历史诊断函数并用确定性正交列掩码切断梯度干扰；(2) 可扩展满秩分支配合微小方差初始化，学习新概念；(3) 形式化表示漂移（RD）以量化历史特征扰动。

Result: 在三个大规模教育数据集上，CLEAN 实现零 RD，对旧题指标与静态锚点完全一致，同时在新题上达到或优于强持续学习基线。

Conclusion: 架构隔离可从结构上保证历史诊断不变性，同时支持概念空间扩展，为增量认知诊断提供兼顾稳定性与可塑性的方案。

Abstract: Cognitive diagnosis (CD) is a fundamental task in intelligent education that profiles learner proficiency over knowledge concepts. In real-world learning platforms, newly added items continually introduce previously unseen concepts, necessitating dynamic expansion of the underlying concept space. Yet existing incremental CD models assume a fixed concept space, allowing gradients from new items to overwrite historical pathways and induce catastrophic forgetting. More critically, these methods rely solely on soft constraints to preserve historical diagnoses. Such constraints may fail to satisfy the requirement of diagnostic invariance after incremental updates, a requirement known as psychometric consistency in cognitive diagnosis. Therefore, we propose CLEAN (Continual Learning with Expandable and Architecturally Isolated Networks), a novel incremental CD framework supporting concept-space expansion while providing structural guarantees for pointwise invariance of historical diagnoses. Specifically, CLEAN first introduces a strict topological bipartition protocol, freezes historical diagnostic functions and applies deterministic orthogonal column masking to sever gradient interference. Second, to accommodate concept expansion, expandable full-rank branches with micro-variance initialization are deployed to learn novel concepts. Finally, to verify that this architectural design achieves invariance by construction, we formalize Representation Drift (RD) to quantify the perturbation of historical traits. Extensive experiments on three large-scale educational datasets demonstrate that CLEAN achieves zero RD, preserving old-item metrics identically to static anchors through architectural isolation while remaining competitive with or superior to strong continual-learning baselines on new items.

</details>


### [55] [MuLoRA: Spectrally Balanced Low-Rank Adaptation for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02283)
*Junkang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MuLoRA，通过历史白化和近似极正交化联合控制LoRA在持续学习中的容量分配与利用，缓解"谱可塑性崩塌"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA的名义秩会掩盖有效适应能力的丧失：序列适应中更新能量集中于少数奇异模态，大量低秩空间未被利用。仅靠避免干扰无法保证剩余适应能力对新任务敏感并被有效利用，即历史表征保护不等于可塑性保留。

Method: MuLoRA包含两部分：1）历史白化，识别相对累积历史响应具有强当前任务响应的输入方向，得到任务自适应基并在训练中固定；2）对动量更新做近似极正交化，降低所选空间内的谱集中。正交基将因子更新谱精确传递到诱导的权重更新。作者给出精确子空间选择的max-min刻画，并在受控跨步各向异性下推导累积谱界。

Result: 在五个类增量基准、八种增量设置下，MuLoRA在报告的16项指标中有15项取得最高平均精度。

Conclusion: MuLoRA通过联合控制LoRA的容量分配与利用，有效缓解谱可塑性崩塌，在多数类增量持续学习设置中优于现有方法。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) provides a parameter-efficient approach to continual learning, but its nominal rank can conceal a loss of effective adaptation capacity. We identify \emph{spectral plasticity collapse}: during sequential adaptation, update energy becomes concentrated in a small subset of singular modes, leaving much of the available low-rank space underutilized. This exposes a limitation of interference avoidance alone: protecting historical representations does not ensure that the remaining adaptation capacity is responsive to new tasks or effectively utilized. To address this problem, we propose \texttt{MuLoRA}, which jointly controls capacity allocation and utilization. First, historical whitening identifies input directions with strong current-task response relative to accumulated historical response, yielding a task-adaptive basis that remains fixed during training. Second, approximate polar orthogonalization of momentum updates reduces spectral concentration within theselected space. An orthonormal basis connects these mechanisms by transferring the factor-update spectrum exactly tothe induced weight update. We establish a max--min characterization of exact subspace selection and derive cumulative spectral bounds under controlled cross-step anisotropy. Across five class-incremental benchmarks and eight incremental settings, \texttt{MuLoRA} achieves the highest mean accuracy in 15 of 16 reported metrics.

</details>


### [56] [Effects of interpulse-interval variation on deep-learning classification of bat vocalizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02284)
*Welmoed R. Eversteijn,Burooj Ghani,A. Leonie Baier,Dan Stowell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究检验蝙蝠脉冲间隔(IPI)对物种分类的贡献，并比较Transformer与CNN模型对该信息的敏感度，结果仅提供有限支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间上下文可能有助于蝙蝠物种自动分类，但具体特征(如IPI)的贡献尚不清楚；同时需检验Transformer是否比CNN更能利用该信息。

Method: 用欧洲蝙蝠录音构建两个匹配数据集：保留原始脉冲时序的自然IPI条件，以及脉冲起点间隔统一为50ms的归一化IPI条件。微调并评估EfficientNet-B0与PaSST；另做跨条件实验(分别训练、在自然IPI测试集评估)；最后评估预训练分类器BatDetect2与BAT。

Result: 条件内IPI归一化效果依赖模型：PaSST在自然IPI(71±2.3%)与归一化IPI(70±6.3%)间差异很小，EfficientNet从47±4.7%升至57±3.9%，且PaSST在两个条件下均优于EfficientNet。跨条件评估中，自然IPI训练模型在自然IPI测试集上更优：EfficientNet由54%降至50%，PaSST由65%降至57%。BatDetect2与BAT在两种条件下差异不大。

Conclusion: 对"自然IPI变异显著贡献蝙蝠物种分类"及"Transformer比CNN更有效利用该信息"的假设支持有限；但跨条件性能下降表明归一化条件下所得结果可能无法完全迁移到自然录音。

Abstract: Temporal context may aid automated bat-species classification, but the contribution of specific features remains unclear. We investigated whether variation in the interpulse interval (IPI)-the time between consecutive call onsets-provides species-discriminative information and whether transformer-based models are more sensitive to this information than convolutional neural networks. We created two matched datasets from European bat recordings: a natural-IPI condition retaining the original call timing and a normalized-IPI condition in which call onsets were spaced at 50-ms intervals. EfficientNet-B0 and PaSST were fine-tuned and evaluated within each condition. In an additional experiment, each architecture was trained separately on natural-IPI and normalized-IPI recordings, and evaluated on the same natural-IPI test set. Finally, the pretrained classifiers BatDetect2 and BAT were evaluated on both conditions. Within-condition IPI normalization had model-dependent effects. PaSST accuracy differed little between the natural-IPI ($71 \pm 2.3\%$) and normalized-IPI ($70 \pm 6.3\%$) conditions, whereas EfficientNet accuracy increased from $47 \pm 4.7\%$ to $57 \pm 3.9\%$. PaSST exceeded EfficientNet under both conditions. In the cross-condition evaluation, models trained on natural-IPI recordings outperformed those trained on normalized-IPI recordings on the natural-IPI test set: accuracy decreased from 54% to 50% for EfficientNet and from 65% to 57% for PaSST. BatDetect2 and BAT differed little between IPI conditions. Overall, we found limited support for the hypotheses that natural IPI variation contributes substantially to bat-species classification and that it is used more effectively by transformer-based than CNN-based models. Nevertheless, the cross-condition performance decrease shows that results obtained under normalized conditions may not transfer fully to natural recordings.

</details>


### [57] [Diffusion-Based Synthetic Data Pretraining for Enhancing Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02292)
*E. Riveros,D. Vega-Oliveros,A. Soriano-Vargas,A. Rocha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在CABiGRU模型基础上，利用扩散模型生成合成传感器数据并进行两阶段训练（合成数据预训练+真实数据微调），在DEO数据集上达到90.6%的平衡准确率，改善了对饮食活动的识别与类别不平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类活动识别（HAR）在医疗健康与日常监测中日益重要，其中饮食活动（如进食、饮水）的检测可为饮食习惯与慢性病管理提供有价值的洞察。然而现有HAR系统在细微且样本不足的类别上表现不佳，限制了其在真实饮食监测中的实用性。

Method: 在CABiGRU（卷积架构结合双向GRU、多头注意力与残差连接，用于处理智能手表加速度计、陀螺仪和磁力计数据）基础上，采用扩散模型生成合成传感器数据窗口，并设计两阶段训练策略：先在合成数据上预训练CABiGRU，再在真实数据上微调。

Result: 在DEO（饮水/进食/其他）数据集上，所提流程取得90.6%的平衡准确率，优于强监督基线，表明基于扩散的合成预训练在识别饮食活动方面具有优势。

Conclusion: 将扩散生成数据与针对性微调相结合，可提升饮食行为识别的鲁棒性，并有效应对类别不平衡问题，有助于在医疗健康与营养监测场景中实现更可靠的部署。

Abstract: Human activity recognition (HAR) is increasingly important for healthcare, well-being, and daily monitoring ap- plications, for which detecting alimentary activities such as eating and drinking can provide actionable insight into dietary habits and chronic disease management. HAR systems, however, often underperform on subtle and underrepresented classes, limiting their utility in real-world dietary monitoring. This work builds upon CABiGRU, a convolutional architecture with Bidirectional GRU layers, multi-head attention, and residual connections, designed to capture discriminative temporal patterns from smart- watch accelerometer, gyroscope, and magnetometer data. To improve CaBiGRU's generalization and reduce underfitting in the minority class, we leverage synthetic sensor data windows using a diffusion model and adopt a two-stage training strategy: pre-training CABiGRU on synthetic data, followed by fine-tuning on the real-world data. On the DEO (drinking/eating/other) dataset, the proposed pipeline achieves a balanced accuracy of 90.6%, improving over a strong supervised baseline and showing the benefits of diffusion-based synthetic pre-training for recognizing alimentary activities and representing a step forward dealing with unbalanced classes. These results suggest that combining diffusion-generated data with targeted fine-tuning enhances robust recognition of dietary behaviors, supporting more reliable deployment in healthcare and nutrition-monitoring settings.

</details>


### [58] [$Ψ$-Resilience: Model-Free Feature Importance from 1D Topological Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02299)
*Fabian Galis,Darian Onchis,Pedro Real Jurado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需模型的$Ψ$-Resilience特征重要性方法，通过1D拓扑信号从数据本身直接生成解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征重要性方法多依赖预测模型，缺乏对分布层面的严谨审计能力，因此需要一种直接从数据本身导出解释、且具备鲁棒性与可审计性的方法。

Method: 构造类别不一致性景观：估计类别条件密度，并沿特征轴取逐点绝对差；对该1D信号计算0维持续同调，定义韧性泛函，仅聚合在用户设定鲁棒性尺度内仍存活的拓扑特征，从而得到上下文鲁棒的重要性分数。

Result: 在带真实重要性标注的合成数据上，方法高保真恢复特征排序，Spearman秩相关最高达0.8，与SHAP、互信息等方法具竞争力；在无真值的真实数据上，与这些方法一致，相关性最高达0.9。

Conclusion: $Ψ$-Resilience是一种稳定的解释方法，可在不依赖预测模型的情况下，实现严格、分布层面的特征重要性审计。

Abstract: We introduce $Ψ$-Resilience, a model-free feature importance method that derives explanations directly from the data itself via 1D topological signals. Our method constructs a class-disagreement landscape by estimating class-conditional densities and taking their pointwise absolute difference along the feature axis. Then, the 0-dimensional persistence of this 1D signal defines a resilience functional that aggregates only those topological features that survive perturbations up to a robustness scale which is set by the user. This gives us a context-robust importance score that is inherently auditable via the underlying 1D landscapes and their persistence. We evaluate our method on both synthetic and real datasets. On synthetic generators with specified ground-truth importance, $Ψ$-Resilience recovers the ranking of features with high fidelity, achieving Spearman rank correlations up to 0.8 and performing competitively with multiple feature importance methods, including SHAP and mutual information. On real datasets with no known ground truth, our technique agrees with these methods, with correlations up to 0.9. These results show that $Ψ$-Resilience is a stable explanation method that enables rigorous, distribution-level auditing of feature importance without relying on a predictive model.

</details>


### [59] [D2K-Bench: Can LLM Agents Turn Expert Designs into Efficient GPU Kernels?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03226)
*Daifeng Li,Huiqiang Jiang,Chengruidong Zhang,Wei Wu,Xudong Guo,Jianhong Tu,Jianwei Zhang,Binhang Yuan,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 D2K-Bench，一个包含26个任务、85个工作负载的诊断性基准，用于衡量LLM智能体将专家设计指导转化为高效GPU内核的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU内核虽然可由LLM智能体生成，但效率往往不及专家实现。仅靠运行时指标无法揭示性能差距与设计发现、实现之间的关系，因此需要一个能诊断性评估智能体如何利用专家设计指导的基准。

Method: 构建D2K-Bench基准（26任务、85工作负载），将指导分为三个层级：L1高层算法洞见、L2数据流设计、L3底层优化技巧，并考虑层级间依赖。采用有/无指导的成对实验，共享任务描述、工作负载、工具、硬件及350轮预算；同时评估独立提出的设计与生成代码中实现的设计属性。在NVIDIA B200 GPU上测试五个模型。

Result: 在130个模型-任务对上，指导使正确率从93.1%提升至98.5%，性能得分从1.46提升至1.95。对三个在两次运行中全部26个任务均正确的模型，几何平均加速比从1.69×提升至2.49×。五个模型的平均综合实现得分从57提升至70（满分100）。

Conclusion: 结果证明专家设计指导具有显著价值，同时识别出仍未实现的设计属性，为未来智能体GPU内核优化指明了改进方向。

Abstract: GPU kernels generated by large language model (LLM) agents can remain less efficient than expert implementations, but runtime alone does not reveal how the gap relates to design discovery and implementation. We introduce D2K-Bench, a diagnostic benchmark of 26 tasks and 85 workloads that measures how effectively agents translate expert design guidance into efficient GPU kernels. The guidance covers L1: high-level algorithmic insights, L2: dataflow design, and L3: low-level optimization tricks, including dependencies among these levels. Pairwise runs with and without guidance share task descriptions, workloads, tools, hardware, and a 350-turn budget. Complementary assessments examine independently proposed designs and the design properties implemented in generated code. Across five models on NVIDIA B200 GPUs, guidance raises correctness over 130 model-task pairs from 93.1% to 98.5% and increases the Performance Score over all 26 tasks from 1.46 to 1.95. For the three frontier models with correct submissions on all 26 tasks in both runs (GPT-6-Astra, Claude-Opus-4.8, and GPT-5.6-Sol), geometric mean speedup increases from $1.69\times$ to $2.49\times$. Across all five models, the mean combined implementation score increases from 57 to 70 out of 100. These results show the value of expert design guidance while identifying design properties that remain unimplemented.

</details>


### [60] [PowerBench: Measuring Language Model Bias in Power-shifting Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02303)
*Nicolas Martorell,Wendy Brau,Gonzalo A. Heredia,Tomás Pablo Korenblit,Gaspar Labastié,Tomás Gimenez Molina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 PowerBench，一个评估语言模型在“权力转移请求”上行为差异的基准，涵盖自我赋权、去权和夺权三类请求及无权力转移的拒绝诱导控制项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型越来越多地协助与权力相关的请求，因此模型在“帮助谁”上的系统性差异可能大规模改变权力分布，或被用户利用来规避拒绝。作者希望让这些不对称性可测量。

Method: 构建并开源一个权力转移请求数据集，变化权力领域、语境、受影响方的规模以及用户的既有权力地位；评估 24 个模型（12 个来自美国开发者、12 个来自中国开发者），在三种实验条件下测试：用户与受影响方国籍互惠、AI 代理作为用户、以及 8 种请求语言。

Result: 模型对夺权的拒绝多于去权，对去权的拒绝多于自我赋权；夺权拒绝率随受影响方规模从个人到社会上升。模型偏向帮助他人从美国夺权，而不愿帮助美国用户从他人夺权，但当美国获益且无人受损时则偏向美国。当用户为 AI 代理时，对权力转移请求的拒绝增加，尤其是在针对个人的夺权上。语言会影响拒绝，但方式因模型而异，平均上大致抵消。

Conclusion: 作者发布 PowerBench，使当前和未来模型中的这些不对称性可被测量。

Abstract: Language models increasingly assist people with power-related requests, so systematic differences in whom they help could shift the distribution of power at scale, or be exploited by users who learn which identities are refused less. We introduce PowerBench, an evaluation of power-shifting requests that distinguishes self-empowerment, disempowerment, and power grabbing, plus a control of refusal-inducing requests that shift no power. We build, curate, and open-source a dataset of such requests varying the power domain, the context, the scale of the affected party, and the prior power standing of the user, and evaluate 24 models (12 from US and 12 from Chinese developers) under three experimental conditions: reciprocal nationalities of user and affected party, an AI agent as the user, and 8 request languages. Models refuse power grabbing more than disempowerment, and disempowerment more than self-empowerment. Refusal of power grabbing rises with the scale of the affected party, from an individual to a society. Models are biased toward helping others take power from the US and against helping US users take power from others, but favor the US when it gains power and nobody loses it. When the user is an AI agent, refusal of power-shifting requests increases, especially in power grabbing against an individual. Finally, language biases refusal, but in model-specific ways that largely cancel on average. We release PowerBench to make these asymmetries measurable in current and future models.

</details>


### [61] [A Path Integral Surrogate for Multi-Step Gradient Inversion in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03597)
*Agnivo Ghosh,Saumik Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PI-SME，将联邦学习中的累积更新视为梯度场的路径积分，用Gauss-Legendre求积在可学习Bézier路径上多个节点近似，从而更准确地重建客户端私有图像，在CIFAR-100和FEMNIST上超越最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习假设客户端共享的模型更新泄露的信息远少于原始训练数据，而梯度反转攻击直接挑战这一前提。FedAvg下客户端更新累积了多个本地训练步骤，服务器只能看到隐藏权重轨迹的两个端点。现有梯度反转攻击沿两端点路径拟合代理模型，但仅在单点读取梯度，信息利用不充分，重建保真度受限。

Method: 提出Path-Integral Surrogate Model Extension（PI-SME）：把累积更新建模为梯度场的路径积分，并通过Gauss-Legendre求积，在一条可学习的Bézier路径上的多个节点处进行近似，从而更充分地刻画整条训练轨迹的梯度信息。

Result: 在CIFAR-100和FEMNIST图像上，覆盖多种轨迹长度和类别受限批次设置，PI-SME在多项反转指标及匹配损失上均比最强的代理模型基线更忠实地重建出私有输入。

Conclusion: 将累积更新视为路径积分并沿可学习路径多点采样，能显著提升梯度反转攻击的重建质量，说明联邦学习中共享更新所隐含的隐私风险比既有单点代理方法所评估的更为严重。

Abstract: Federated learning lets many clients train a shared model together without ever sending their private data to a central server. Each client shares only a model update, and this update should reveal far less about the client than its raw training examples would. This premise is what protects the privacy of the clients. Gradient inversion attacks challenge it directly by trying to reconstruct a client's private input images from the single update it shared. Under FedAvg, a client's update accumulates several local training steps, so the server sees only the two endpoints of a hidden weight trajectory. Recent gradient inversion attacks fit a surrogate model along the path between these two endpoints but they still read its gradient at a single point. We propose the Path-Integral Surrogate Model Extension (PI-SME) which treats the accumulated update as a path integral of the gradient field and approximates it by Gauss--Legendre quadrature over several nodes along a learnable Bézier path. On CIFAR-100 and FEMNIST images across a range of trajectory lengths and class-restricted batches PI-SME reconstructs the private inputs more faithfully than the strongest surrogate baseline on several inversion metrics and the matching loss.

</details>


### [62] [Slow-Fast Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation for Capability Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02324)
*Xiaofei Yin,Tong Chu,Jiyuan Fu,Jun Lan,Shuheng Zhou,Huijia Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SF-MOPD，通过快慢模型耦合缓解多教师在线策略蒸馏中的能力干扰问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多教师在线策略蒸馏（MOPD）能将多领域专家知识整合到单一学生模型中，但训练中学生逐渐偏离初始化模型，随着位移增大，通用能力下降，产生能力干扰。简单地将学生约束回初始化又会抑制领域专业知识的习得。

Method: 提出慢快多教师在线策略蒸馏（SF-MOPD），将直接由各教师更新的当前学生作为快模型，将学生的指数移动平均（EMA）作为慢模型。慢模型逐步吸收学习信号，充当融合通用基础与已确认领域专业知识的动态能力参考。对每个教师，在log概率空间中计算教师诱导的更新，仅移除使快模型进一步远离慢模型的分量，保留对齐和正交分量。

Result: 在多个模型规模上的实验表明，SF-MOPD有效缓解能力干扰，增强专门的多模态能力，并减少通用能力基准上的平均退化，一致优于原始MOPD。

Conclusion: SF-MOPD通过快慢模型机制在获取领域专业知识与保持通用能力之间取得平衡，是一种有效缓解多教师蒸馏能力干扰的方法。

Abstract: Foundation multimodal large language models are designed to support a broad spectrum of capabilities across diverse domains. Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) provides an effective framework for consolidating domain-specific expertise into a single student model. However, MOPD training gradually drives the student away from its initialization model, and general capabilities decline as the displacement grows, resulting in capability interference. A direct remedy is constraining the student toward its initialization, but this suppresses the acquisition of domain expertise as well. We propose Slow-Fast Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation (SF-MOPD), which couples a fast model, the current student updated directly by each teacher, with a slow model, an exponential moving average of the student. The slow model absorbs the learning signal gradually, serving as a moving capability reference that fuses the general foundation with confirmed domain expertise. For each teacher, SF-MOPD computes the teacher-induced update in log-probability space and removes only the component that pushes the fast model further away from the slow model, while retaining aligned and orthogonal components. Experiments across multiple model scales demonstrate that SF-MOPD effectively mitigates capability interference, enhances specialized multimodal capabilities, and reduces the average degradation on general-capability benchmarks, consistently outperforming vanilla MOPD.

</details>


### [63] [Drive vs. Decay: On the Training Dynamics of Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02344)
*José Lucas De Melo Costa,Seong Woo Ahn,Fabrice Popineau,Arpad Rimmel,Bich-Liên Doan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出JEPA早期训练稳定性理论，用"驱动-衰减"竞争解释表征坍塌，并据此设计ResidualPred预测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA易发生表征坍塌，现有缓解手段多为经验性启发式，缺乏统一的理论解释。

Method: 在平凡不动点附近线性化耦合的JEPA梯度流，得到驱动效应γ与衰减效应σ；在近似谱解耦下定义逐模态稳定比μ_i=γ_i/σ_i，其可分解为数据侧与预测器侧独立项，不稳定模态数对应可涌现表征的秩。据此推导相变边界，并将预测器缩放、掩码比例、EMA统一为移动μ的机制；进而提出ResidualPred——初始化时注意力偏向恒等映射的Transformer预测器。

Result: 在超过800组Tabular-JEPA配置中验证了预测的相变边界；ResidualPred在表格基准及CIFAR-10/100、STL-10、ImageNet的I-JEPA预训练中同时提升有效秩与下游准确率。

Conclusion: 该框架将经验性防坍塌启发式与显式动力学图景相连接，给出了理论驱动的稳定器。代码已开源。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) are prone to representation collapse, typically mitigated through empirical heuristics. We develop an early-training stability theory that unifies these heuristics. Linearising the coupled JEPA gradient flow around the trivial fixed point reveals two competing effects: a driving force ($γ$) and a decay effect ($σ$). Under approximate spectral decoupling, a per-mode stability ratio $μ_i = γ_i / σ_i$ factorises into independent data-side and predictor-side terms and the count of unstable modes tracks the rank of representations that can emerge. The framework predicts a phase boundary, which we confirm empirically across more than 800 Tabular-JEPA configurations. It also unifies predictor scaling, masking ratio, and EMA as distinct mechanisms for shifting $μ$. Guided by this analysis, we introduce ResidualPred, a transformer predictor whose attention is biased toward the identity at initialisation; it improves both effective rank and downstream accuracy on tabular benchmarks and in I-JEPA pretraining on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, STL-10, and ImageNet. Our framework connects empirical collapse-avoidance heuristics to an explicit dynamical picture, yielding theory-driven stabilizers. Code is available at https://github.com/jose-melo/drive-vs-decay.

</details>


### [64] [Mitigating Convergence Collapse in Fixed-Target Anomaly Detectors via Kernel-Anchored Locality Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02345)
*José Lucas De Melo Costa,Fabrice Popineau,Arpad Rimmel,Bich-Liên Doan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 一类固定目标的表格异常检测器在平方误差损失下训练神经映射，并用测试残差打分；我们揭示其"收敛崩溃"现象——优化越好，检测越差，因为它们依赖隐性不收敛来保留信号，并据此提出核锚定正则化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文关注一类表格异常检测器（收缩匹配、一步整流流、重建自编码器），它们在平方误差损失下将神经映射训练至固定目标，并以测试时残差作为异常分数。作者发现一个反直觉的"收敛崩溃"：优化得越充分，检测性能反而越差。因为收敛后学习到的映射在分布外也紧跟目标，导致残差信号在异常与正常数据上都消失，这类检测器只能依赖早停、容量限制等隐性不收敛手段维持信号。作者认为这是结构性问题，异常检测需要能阻止无约束外推的"局部性约束"，而经典检测器（kNN、KDE、孤立森林、LOF）显式具备这一约束，固定目标神经检测器却没有。因此作者希望从理论上形式化这一联系，并给出可行的补救方案。

Method: 作者先从理论上建立联系：证明固定目标检测器的核回归类比是一个有限带宽的 Nadaraya-Watson 平滑器，称为 Kernel Contraction Matching（KCM）。KCM 具有闭式解、无需训练、CPU 高效的特点。基于这一桥梁，作者进一步提出 Kernel-Anchored Regularizer（KAR），它惩罚神经预测与训练目标核加权平均之间的偏离，从而为神经检测器注入显式的局部性约束。

Result: KCM 无需训练且仅用 CPU 即可高效计算，却在 ADBench 上匹配了已有的神经基线。KAR 在易发生崩溃的 ADBench 数据集与三种骨干网络上，缓解了收敛崩溃问题，并在延长训练时提升了 AUROC。

Conclusion: 固定目标神经异常检测器的收敛崩溃是结构性的，根源在于缺乏局部性约束；经典检测器的局部性正是其有效性来源。通过核回归桥梁（KCM）可将这一约束显式化，而 KAR 作为实用的正则化手段，能够在不牺牲神经模型表达能力的前提下抑制崩溃、提升长期训练下的检测性能。

Abstract: A family of tabular anomaly detectors trains a neural map toward a fixed target under squared-error loss and scores anomalies by the test-time residual; contraction matching, one-step rectified flow, and reconstruction autoencoders all fit this template. We characterize a convergence collapse: better optimization makes the detector worse. At convergence, the learned map tracks the target even off-distribution, so the residual signal vanishes on anomalies as well as on normal data. These detectors therefore rely on implicit non-convergence (early stopping, capacity caps) to retain signal. We argue this is structural: effective anomaly detection requires a locality constraint that blocks unconstrained extrapolation. Classical detectors (kNN, KDE, isolation forests, LOF) enforce locality explicitly; fixed-target neural detectors do not. We formalize the connection by showing that the kernel-regression analog of a fixed-target detector is a finite-bandwidth Nadaraya-Watson smoother, which we call Kernel Contraction Matching (KCM). KCM is closed-form, training-free, and CPU-efficient, yet matches established neural baselines on ADBench. Building on this bridge, we introduce the Kernel-Anchored Regularizer (KAR), which penalizes deviation of the neural prediction from a kernel-weighted average of training targets. Across collapse-prone ADBench datasets and three backbones, KAR mitigates collapse and improves AUROC under prolonged training.

</details>


### [65] [From Behavior to Provenance: Attributing Tabular Foundation Models to Synthetic Pretraining Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02347)
*Mohamed Bouadi,Nassim Bouarour,Shivam Dubey,Aditya Tanna,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用受控合成预训练数据（O'PRIOR 生成器）构建可实验验证的训练数据归因测试平台，通过行为条件归因、反事实重训与来源干预，验证归因的任务级忠实性与机制级一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练数据归因旨在识别哪些训练样本塑造了模型行为，但由于因果性训练影响难以观测，这类归因主张很难被验证。作者因此主张用可控的合成预训练使归因可被实验检验。

Method: 使用 O'PRIOR（面向表格基础模型的、来源丰富的合成任务生成器）构建测试床，每个预训练任务对结构机制、缺失性、混杂、捷径和分布偏移都带有显式血缘标注。结合行为条件归因、反事实重训与来源感知干预，同时检验任务级忠实性与机制级一致性。

Result: 在留出的真实任务上，移除归因排名前 5% 的合成任务使平均 ROC-AUC 下降 0.013，而随机移除仅下降 0.002±0.004；移除归因最低的任务反而提升性能 0.003。在捷径血缘任务中，定向移除效应为 0.043，匹配随机移除为 0.016。来源判别较弱，排序 AUROC 仅 0.55–0.62，尽管 top-k 富集显著，说明来源关联与干预忠实性未必一致。

Conclusion: 结果表明合成来源可作为可验证的贡献性归因的受控设定，并揭示来源关联与干预忠实性可能相互分离。

Abstract: Training-data attribution aims to identify which training examples shape model behavior, yet validating such claims is difficult because causal training influence is rarely observable. We argue that controlled synthetic pretraining makes attribution experimentally testable. Using O'PRIOR, a provenance-rich synthetic task generator for tabular foundation models, we construct a testbed in which every pretraining task carries explicit lineage over structural mechanisms, missingness, confounding, shortcuts, and distribution shift. We combine behavior-conditioned attribution with counterfactual retraining and provenance-aware interventions to test both task-level faithfulness and mechanism-level consistency. On held-out real tasks, removing the top-attributed 5% of synthetic tasks decreases mean ROC-AUC by 0.013, compared with 0.002$\pm$0.004 under random removal, while removing bottom-attributed tasks improves performance by 0.003. Within shortcut-provenance tasks, targeted removal yields an effect of 0.043 versus 0.016 for matched random removal. Provenance discrimination is more modest by ranking AUROC (0.55-0.62), despite substantial top-k enrichment, revealing that provenance association and interventional faithfulness need not coincide. Our results establish synthetic provenance as a controlled setting for verifiable contributive attribution

</details>


### [66] [Does Every User Need a Private LoRA? Decoupling Personalization from Per-User Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02353)
*Songyuan Sui,Srikanth Malla,Chiho Choi,Joon Hee Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出LINEUP，通过共享可重用低秩个性化因子+用户条件召回与查询校准，将每个用户的可训练状态压缩到8个标量，实现高效个性化大语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化大语言模型通常需要为每个用户维护完整的适配状态，随用户规模增长扩展性差。论文从"个性化容量分配"视角重新审视该设计：多少适配容量可跨用户共享、共享容量如何组成、多少必须保持用户专属。

Method: 基于三项实证分析发现：独立用户适配器含大量跨用户可复用结构；可复用方向的价值取决于用户相关性与查询间差异；用户历史提供可迁移信号用于紧凑个体校正。据此提出LINEUP：学习可重用低秩个性化因子库，通过用户条件召回与查询相关校准组合，并将目标用户适配限制为共享校正空间上的极小用户码（每人仅优化8个标量，所有共享组件固定）。

Result: 理论分析给出有限步、有限历史的风险界，以及用户码细化优于历史初始化的充分条件。在个性化分类、预测、生成共六项任务、12个指标上均领先（各指标为三次独立运行均值），例如相对最强基线将LaMP-3 RMSE降低11.4%，且在历史有限时仍保持优势。对比的私有LoRA配置每用户使用419万参数。

Conclusion: 丰富的个性化主要可由可重用、条件组合的共享容量支撑，而独立用户适配可局限于极小的校正状态，从而将表达性个性化容量与每用户可训练状态解耦。

Abstract: Personalized large language models often require a complete adaptation state for each user. However, this paradigm scales poorly as the user population grows. We revisit this design through the lens of personalization capacity allocation: how much adaptation capacity can be shared across users, how the shared capacity should be composed, and how much must remain user-specific. We answer them through three complementary empirical analyses. We find that independent user adapters contain substantial cross-user reusable structure, that the utility of reusable directions reflects both user relevance and variation across queries, and that user histories provide transferable signals for compact individual correction. Motivated by these findings, we propose LINEUP. It learns a bank of reusable low-rank personalization factors, composes them through user-conditioned recall and query-dependent calibration, and restricts target-user adaptation to a tiny user code over a shared correction space. This design decouples expressive personalization capacity from per-user trainable state. Each target user optimizes only eight scalars, while all shared components remain fixed. By comparison, the evaluated private-LoRA configuration uses 4.19 million per-user parameters. Our theoretical analysis gives a finite-step, finite-history risk bound and sufficient conditions for user-code refinement to improve on history initialization. Across six tasks spanning personalized classification, prediction, and generation, LINEUP leads on all 12 metrics, each averaged over three independent runs (e.g., reducing LaMP-3 RMSE by 11.4% relative to the strongest baseline). It maintains advantages under limited history. These results show that rich personalization can be supported primarily by reusable, conditionally composed shared capacity, while independent user adaptation remains confined to a tiny correction state.

</details>


### [67] [Why Does Adaptive Batching Help LLM Pretraining? A Perspective from Unbounded Variance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02355)
*Arda Fazla,Antesh Upadhyay,Ege C. Kaya,M. Berk Sahin,Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出广义BG-$a$噪声模型，刻画LLM预训练中梯度方差随距离初始化的增长行为，证明$Ω(ε^{-(4+a)})$下界并给出匹配上界，并提出自适应批量大小调度器，在OLMo2预训练中取得更低验证损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM预训练中增大批量大小是常见做法，但其理论依据不清晰。随机优化分析常假设梯度方差一致有界，然而该假设在许多实际非凸问题中并不成立。BG-$0$噪声模型虽放宽了假设（方差随距初始化距离二次增长），但其增长在实际中可能过于保守。

Method: 首先实证研究LLM预训练中的方差增长，发现带可调增长指数的广义BG模型能更紧致地描述实际噪声行为。据此提出广义BG-$a$噪声模型，在有限方差($a=0$)与BG-$0$($a=2$)之间插值。在$L$-光滑性假设下推导信息论下界，并通过随迭代远离初始化而增大批量大小建立匹配的上界。最后提出通过动态调整批量大小控制方差增长的自适应批量调度器。

Result: 理论上得到依赖增长的oracle复杂度下界$Ω(ε^{-(4+a)})$及在$ε$依赖上匹配的上界。在C4数据集上预训练最多1B参数的OLMo2模型时，该调度器在相同token预算下取得比小批量和大批量训练更低的验证损失，且迭代次数不到小批量训练的10%。

Conclusion: 广义BG-$a$噪声模型更准确地刻画实际噪声行为，据此设计的自适应批量调度器能有效控制方差增长，在LLM预训练中兼具更低验证损失与更高迭代效率，为批量大小调度提供了理论与实践依据。

Abstract: Increasing the batch size during training is a common practice in large language model (LLM) pretraining, yet the theoretical justification behind its success is not well understood. Analyses of stochastic optimization often assume uniformly bounded stochastic gradient variance, yet recent evidence suggests that this assumption fails in many practical nonconvex problems. The Blum--Gladyshev (BG-$0$) noise model relaxes this assumption by allowing the variance to grow quadratically with the distance from initialization, suggesting that batch size schedulers can help by controlling the variance growth during training. However, this growth can be overly conservative in practice. We empirically investigate variance growth in LLM pretraining and observe that a generalized BG model with a tunable growth exponent provides a tighter description of practical noise behavior. Motivated by this observation, we introduce the generalized BG-$a$ noise model, which interpolates between bounded variance ($a=0$) and BG-$0$ noise ($a=2$). Under $L$-smoothness, we derive an information-theoretic lower bound with growth-dependent oracle complexity $Ω(ε^{-(4+a)})$ and establish a matching upper bound in $ε$-dependence by increasing the batch size as the iterates move away from initialization. Finally, we propose an adaptive batch scheduler that controls variance growth through dynamic batch size adjustments during training. In pretraining OLMo2 models of up to 1B parameters on C4, our scheduler achieves a lower validation loss than both small and large batch training under matched token budgets, while using less than 10\% of the iterations of small batch training.

</details>


### [68] [Lexicographic Multi-Objective On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02359)
*Doseok Jang,Jon Ander Campos,Youran Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 LMOPD，一种按显式优先级整合多奖励专家策略的多教师蒸馏方法，在数学基准上比基线更有效地保留高优先级能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR 通常只优化答案正确性，但有用模型还需高质量推理与简洁回答。现有多奖励后训练方法通过标量化或简单组合专家，未显式保护奖励优先级顺序，而权衡是不对称的（如简洁性不应牺牲正确性）。

Method: 对每个学生 rollout，按优先级选择第一个其门控检测到缺陷的目标对应的专家；再将其中心化对数策略修正做局部投影，去除与更高优先级专家相冲突的分量。

Result: 在 30B-A3B MoE 模型上，两专家设置完全保留准确率与推理质量增益并获 46.9% 简洁性增益；四专家设置保留约 90% 准确率与推理正确性增益，而最佳基线仅约 57%；消融显示字典序路由优于随机路由，投影进一步增强最高优先级能力。

Conclusion: 在两种规模上，LMOPD 均比所评估基线更好地保留最高优先级能力，表明显式优先级对专家整合具有价值。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) usually optimizes answer correctness, yet useful language-model behavior also requires high-quality reasoning and concise responses. Existing multi-reward post-training methods typically scalarize rewards or combine specialists without explicitly protecting a reward priority order. This is problematic when trade-offs are asymmetric: conciseness, for example, should not improve at the cost of correctness. We introduce Lexicographic Multi-Objective On-Policy Distillation (LMOPD), a multi-teacher method for integrating reward-specialized policies under explicit priorities. For each student rollout, LMOPD selects the specialist for the first objective whose gate detects a deficiency, then locally projects its centered log-policy correction to remove components that oppose higher-priority specialists. We evaluate 30B-A3B mixture-of-experts transformer models in two- and four-expert settings on three math benchmarks, measuring retained specialist gains. With two experts, LMOPD's point estimates fully retain the accuracy and reasoning-quality gains while acquiring $46.9\%$ of the conciseness gain. With four experts, it retains $\approx90\%$ of both the accuracy gain and reasoning-correctness gain, compared to only $\approx57\%$ by the next best evaluated baseline. Matched four-expertablations show that lexicographic routing outperforms random routing and that projection further strengthens both top-priority capabilities.
