Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models combine learned routing with selection from a large expert pool. We isolate the contribution of dynamic expert combination using a dense analogue: $K$ SwiGLU experts, all active for every token and combined by a softmax gate, at fixed total FFN width. With no larger pool or discrete selection, the dense baseline is the $K=1$ case. Validation loss varies non-monotonically with $K$: $K=2$ improves over the baseline by $0.0048$, whereas $K=4$ and $K=6$ worsen it by $0.0053$ and $0.0197$, respectively. Routing generally remains soft and expert usage balanced, except in the first layer of the $K=4$ model, where routing is nearly one-hot. Forcing this gate to uniform increases loss by $2.4$ nats on a diagnostic subset, indicating that its concentrated routing is functionally important. We further show that the architecture is exactly a dense SwiGLU with token-dependent, simplex-constrained group scaling and contains the dense baseline in its function class. These single-run results suggest a granularity sweet spot for always-active, softly gated FFNs at fixed width, with $K=2$ performing best among the configurations tested.

</details>


### [105] [Fisher-Guided Submodular Data Selection for Continual Pre-Training of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02593)
*Zhenghao Zhao,Gaowen Liu,Zhiling Lan,Yan Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于Fisher信息的持续预训练数据选择方法，通过分解梯度中的锚定分量与前沿分量，在不增加通用领域回放数据的前提下，同时提升目标域效果并抑制灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在持续预训练（CPT）中，网络规模语料噪声大、token预算有限，数据选择成为核心瓶颈。选定目标域语料不当会覆盖预训练模型中已编码的能力，导致灾难性遗忘。现有做法要么用困惑度等与参数无关的标量来打分，要么耗费大量通用领域回放token来缓解遗忘，二者都未直接考量"在候选数据上训练将如何改变模型参数"。

Method: 作者发现基于损失的数据选择会使CPT后的Fisher对角在预训练模型已承诺的高Fisher坐标上下降，而低Fisher坐标基本不变，由此揭示灾难性遗忘的参数空间机制。据此提出Fisher感知的CPT选择器：将每个候选样本的梯度分解为锚定分量（衡量沿已承诺参数方向的扰动）与前沿分量（衡量在未受约束的低Fisher子空间中的更新能力），用对数行列式子模目标聚合这些信号，并通过可扩展的流式数据选择流水线单遍优化。

Result: 在TinyLlama-1.1B和Llama-3.1-8B上针对医学数据做CPT时，该选择器在提升目标域质量的同时，将留出预训练基准上的遗忘控制在有限范围内。其token效率显著优于遗忘感知回放：1B个被选token在适应性和遗忘两方面均超过用10B token训练的replay策略，带来10倍的token效率优势。

Conclusion: 该工作表明数据选择应关注训练对参数的影响而非仅依赖参数无关的标量指标，Fisher感知的子模选择能在CPT中同时兼顾目标域适应与遗忘抑制，并大幅降低token成本。

Abstract: Data selection is already a central bottleneck in large-language-model training, where web-scale corpora are noisy and token budgets are finite. In continual pre-training (CPT), it becomes a forgetting-control problem: a poorly chosen target-domain corpus can overwrite capabilities encoded in the pretrained checkpoint. Existing CPT practice either scores candidates with parameter-agnostic scalars such as perplexity, or mitigates forgetting by spending many extra general-domain replay tokens. Neither strategy directly asks how training on a candidate will move the model parameters. We show that loss-based selection causes the post-CPT Fisher diagonal to drift downward on exactly the high-Fisher coordinates the pretrained model had committed to, while leaving low-Fisher coordinates largely untouched. This asymmetry exposes a parameter-space mechanism for catastrophic forgetting. Motivated by this observation, we propose a Fisher-aware CPT selector that decomposes each candidate's gradient into an anchor component, which measures perturbation along committed parameter directions, and a frontier component, which measures update capacity in unconstrained low-Fisher subspaces. We aggregate these signals with a log-determinant submodular objective and optimize it in a single pass using a scalable streaming data selection pipeline. On TinyLlama-1.1B and Llama-3.1-8B CPT over medical data, our selector improves target-domain quality while bounding forgetting on held-out pretraining benchmarks. Most importantly, it is substantially more token-efficient than forgetting-aware replay. 1B selected tokens already outperform the replay strategy trained with 10B tokens on both adaptation and forgetting, giving a 10x token-efficiency advantage.

</details>


### [106] [How Causality Bridges the Semantic Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02594)
*Shuhao Zhang,Xuran Zhou,Han Guo,Pengtao Xie,Yujia Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CausalBridge框架，利用因果结构而非通用人类知识为无标签变量赋予语义，通过从测量数据中发现因果图并求解嵌入，再经语言模型表达为名称，在问卷和机器人场景中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值测量捕获了系统行为，但变量的含义常未标明：有的变量被测量却无标签，有的从未被测量。现有方法依赖通用人类知识赋予语义，这既继承了人类知识存在的偏见，又在知识缺失处无能为力。作者希望弥合测量与含义之间的鸿沟，改用因果结构作为信息来源，因为因果结构反映了生成测量的机制、仅从测量中恢复，可能是唯一不受人类知识偏见影响的信息源。

Method: 形式化为"结构约束的语义对齐"（structure-constrained semantic alignment）：每个无名变量的嵌入在因果图所蕴含的依赖关系下求解，以少量已知名称的嵌入作为锚点。构建CausalBridge框架：从测量数据中发现因果图（包含潜变量），在这些关系下求解嵌入，再通过语言模型将嵌入表达为名称。

Result: 在五份问卷和三个机器人场景上评估，20%至90%的变量名被掩蔽。相比依赖关联的现有方法，CausalBridge更准确地恢复观测变量和潜变量的语义，且系统文档越少时优势越大。其发现的因果图命名变量的准确率与已记录的因果图相当；命名一个新系统只需数分钟，成本仅为采样方法的一小部分。

Conclusion: 一旦语义鸿沟被忠实弥合，机器就能理解世界并以因果方式采取行动。

Abstract: Numerical measurements capture how a system behaves, but often leave the meanings of its variables unspecified. Some variables are measured but never labeled, and others are never measured at all. Existing methods assign semantics to such variables by consulting general human knowledge, but this inherits its biases where that knowledge exists and offers nothing where it does not. We bridge this gap between measurements and their meanings with causal structure instead, reading a variable's semantics from how it acts on other variables. We formalize this as structure-constrained semantic alignment, in which the embedding of each unnamed variable is solved under the dependence relations implied by the causal graph, with the embeddings of a few known names as anchors. Accordingly, we build CausalBridge, a framework that discovers the causal graph from the measurements, latent variables included, solves for the embeddings under those relations, and expresses them as names through a language model. The causal structure reflects the mechanism that generated the measurements and is recovered from the measurements alone, which may make it the one source of information free of bias from human knowledge. We evaluate CausalBridge on five questionnaires and three robotics scenarios, with 20 to 90% of the variable names masked. It recovers the semantics of observed and latent variables more accurately than existing methods that rely on association, and its lead widens as less of the system is documented. The graph it discovers names variables as accurately as the documented one, and a new system is named in minutes and at a fraction of the cost of sampling methods. Once the semantic gap is bridged faithfully, machines can understand the world and take actions causally.

</details>


### [107] [Activation Sparsity with Weight Approximation for Faster LLM Decoding on Offloaded Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02598)
*JuneHyung Kim,Sankeerth Durvasula,Nandita Vijaykumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SpAx 将"读/不读"权重的二选一改为"保留/压缩近似/跳过"三选一，从而在利用激活稀疏时更好地平衡模型质量与解码速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在消费级 GPU 上部署 LLM 时，显存不足以容纳全部权重，解码需要反复从系统 RAM 或闪存把被卸载的权重搬入 GPU，带宽远低于本地显存访问，导致推理极慢。激活稀疏可跳过与零/近零激活相关的权重以减少传输，但省略的激活贡献越多，模型质量会急剧下降，说明小幅度激活对应的权重会集中影响质量。因此需要改善激活稀疏下模型质量与解码性能之间的权衡。

Method: SpAx 用三种处理方式替代是否读取权重的二元选择：完全保留、用压缩权重表示近似、或完全省略。具体地，跳过与最接近零的激活相关的权重；对较小幅度激活读取近似权重；对最大幅度激活读取原始权重。较小幅度激活会衰减近似权重带来的误差，而压缩权重表示需要的传输字节更少。

Result: 权重卸载到 CPU 内存时，SpAx 平均加速解码 3.86 倍（最高 5.57 倍，16 位权重）、2.06 倍（最高 2.74 倍，4 位权重），WikiText-2 困惑度增加最多 10%。权重卸载到闪存时，加速为平均 3.31 倍（最高 4.81 倍）和 1.54 倍（最高 2.03 倍）。

Conclusion: 通过引入"保留—近似—跳过"的三选一机制，SpAx 在激活稀疏条件下显著提升了卸载权重场景的解码速度，同时将模型质量损失控制在可接受范围内。

Abstract: Deploying LLMs on consumer-grade GPUs with insufficient memory to hold their weights can result in prohibitively slow inference, because decoding repeatedly transfers offloaded weights from system RAM or flash storage into GPU at much lower bandwidth than local GPU-memory access. Activation sparsity reduces these transfers by skipping weights associated with zero or near-zero activations. However, as more activation contributions are omitted, model quality eventually degrades rapidly, indicating that weights associated with small-magnitude activations collectively influence model quality sharply. In this work, we improve the trade-off between model quality and decoding performance when exploiting activation sparsity. Our key idea is to replace the binary choice of whether or not to read a weight with three options: fully retain it, approximate it using a compressed weight representation, or omit it entirely. SpAx skips weights associated with activations closest to zero, reads approximate weights for smaller-magnitude activations, and reads original weights for the largest-magnitude activations. Smaller-magnitude activations attenuate the errors introduced by approximate weights, while compressed weight representations require fewer bytes to be transferred. With weights offloaded to CPU memory, SpAx speeds up decoding by 3.86X on average (up to 5.57X) with 16-bit weights and 2.06X (up to 2.74X) with 4-bit weights, at a WikiText-2 perplexity increase of at most 10%. With weights offloaded to flash storage, the speedups are 3.31X on average (up to 4.81X) and 1.54X (up to 2.03X).

</details>


### [108] [Seer: Maximum Likelihood Regression for Learning-Speed Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02610)
*Carl Myers Kadie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Seer 系统，通过对分类学习性能进行经验观测并建立统计模型，来预测达到目标精度所需的训练样本数以及无限样本下的最大精度，并在三个真实领域验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习性能的预测对于实践者而言十分重要，但缺乏既能刻画学习曲线又具备实用参数与高效求解方法的模型。作者希望建立能体现分类学习最优约束的最大似然模型，从而可靠地预测学习性能。

Method: 首先梳理良好最大似然分类学习性能模型的要求，探索合理的设计选择；将模型选择问题视为非线性规划，利用问题约束将其化简为可用高效迭代算法求解的非线性回归任务；随后在黄豆病害、心脏病和听力学三个领域开展近 100 个实验进行评估。

Result: 实验表明 Seer 能很好地刻画学习性能，并且在预测学习性能方面似乎已达到尽可能好的水平；论文还给出了针对具体情境选择回归模型的建议。

Conclusion: Seer 通过结合合理约束的模型与高效的最大似然求解算法，实现了对分类学习性能的有效建模与预测，具有实际应用价值，并指出了未来研究方向。

Abstract: The research presented here focuses on modeling machine-learning performance. The thesis introduces Seer, a system that generates empirical observations of classification-learning performance and then uses those observations to create statistical models. The models can be used to predict the number of training examples needed to achieve a desired level and the maximum accuracy possible given an unlimited number of training examples. Seer advances the state of the art with 1) models that embody the best constraints for classification learning and most useful parameters, 2) algorithms that efficiently find maximum-likelihood models, and 3) a demonstration on real-world data from three domains of a practicable application of such modeling.
  The first part of the thesis gives an overview of the requirements for a good maximum-likelihood model of classification-learning performance. Next, reasonable design choices for such models are explored. Selection among such models is a task of nonlinear programming, but by exploiting appropriate problem constraints, the task is reduced to a nonlinear regression task that can be solved with an efficient iterative algorithm. The latter part of the thesis describes almost 100 experiments in the domains of soybean disease, heart disease, and audiological problems. The tests show that Seer is excellent at characterizing learning-performance and that it seems to be as good as possible at predicting learning performance. Finally, recommendations for choosing a regression model for a particular situation are made and directions for further research are identified.

</details>


### [109] [Scale-Recursive Rectified Flows for Few-Step Precipitation Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02611)
*Shunya Nagashima,Takumi Bannai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出尺度递归整流流模型，通过将采样步数更多分配给粗尺度流以提升降水降尺度集合的概率精度与检出率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率降水估计对洪水风险评估和水资源管理很重要，但粗分辨率卫星产品无法刻画网格内的降水差异。生成式模型可用集合成员表达这种不确定性，其中整流流通过迭代把噪声变换为降水场；减少采样步数可加速生成，却会使集合成员过于相似、低估不确定性。

Method: 提出尺度递归整流流（scale-recursive rectified flow）：先生成大尺度降水格局，再生成局部细节；通过比较各空间尺度上的集合变异性与预测误差来指导采样步数分配，并利用验证分数与降水功率谱约束该分配以避免过度放大。

Result: 在美国本土的卫星到雷达降尺度任务中，分析发现大尺度降水格局是采样预算受限时集合变异性不足的主要来源。在固定架构与计算成本下，将更多步数分配给粗尺度流，可在不同训练随机种子下提升概率精度和降水检出能力；相比使用更多步数的非递归流，该模型以更短的采样时间取得更好的概率精度。

Conclusion: 按空间尺度递归分配采样步数、重点保障粗尺度流的采样预算，是缓解降尺度集合变异性不足、兼顾精度与效率的有效途径。

Abstract: Fine-resolution precipitation estimates support flood risk assessment and water management, but coarse satellite products cannot resolve rainfall within each grid cell. Generative models address this ambiguity by producing ensembles of plausible high-resolution rainfall fields. Among these models, rectified flows generate samples by iteratively transforming random noise into rainfall fields. Reducing the number of sampling steps accelerates generation but can make ensemble members too similar, understating uncertainty. We propose a scale-recursive rectified flow that generates broad patterns before local details and guides sampling-step allocation by comparing ensemble variability with prediction error across spatial scales. Validation scores and rainfall power spectra constrain the allocation to avoid excessive amplification. In satellite-to-radar downscaling over the contiguous United States, our analysis identified broad rainfall patterns as the main source of insufficient ensemble variability under reduced sampling budgets. Allocating more steps to the coarse flow improved probabilistic accuracy and rain detection across training seeds at fixed architecture and computational cost. The proposed model also achieved better probabilistic accuracy with shorter sampling time than a nonrecursive flow using more steps.

</details>


### [110] [What Is Lost in Post-Training? Default Collapse and the Loss of In-Context Steerability Across Diverse Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02614)
*Jessica Dierking,Itai Shapira,Niclas Boehmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 后训练不仅会收窄模型立场，还会削弱其根据上下文被引导到未受训练偏好的观点的能力，揭示单一价值优先与多元服务能力之间的张力，并提出立场分布匹配的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 模型需服务异质人群，须依用户与情境采取恰当原则。既有研究关注后训练对模型默认表达的收窄，却忽视了其上下文适应能力，因此本文探究后训练对在上下文中被引导能力的影响。

Method: 在受控实验中，将模型微调至文化价值分歧中的某一侧，并在训练全程评估各检查点，考察训练侧立场的主导化及对相反立场识别与执行的衰退；随后提出并分析在表达立场满足给定分布约束下最大化奖励的替代目标，并以立场分布匹配作为实践实现。

Result: 训练侧在常规使用中日益占优，而识别并忠实呈现对立观点的能力随之下降，表明后训练在强化单一价值取向的同时损害了上下文中被引导的能力。

Conclusion: 优先单一价值与保留服务多元利益相关者所需的技术能力之间存在张力；所提出的立场分布匹配等替代目标为兼顾两者提供了可行方向。

Abstract: AI models serving a heterogeneous population must act on the principles appropriate to each user and context. While post-training has been shown to narrow the views large language models express, prior work has focused on default behavior rather than the ability to adapt to in-context information. We show that post-training also degrades a model's ability to be steered in-context toward perspectives it was not trained to favor. In controlled experiments, we fine-tune models toward one side of cultural-value disagreements and evaluate checkpoints throughout training. The trained side becomes increasingly dominant in ordinary use, while the ability to recognize and faithfully enact the opposing view declines. These findings point to a tension between prioritizing a single set of values and preserving the technical capacity needed to serve diverse stakeholders. Finally, we propose and analyze an alternative objective that maximizes reward subject to a prescribed distribution over expressed perspectives, and present stance-distribution matching as a practical implementation.

</details>


### [111] [Quantifying the Value of Constructive Induction, Knowledge, and Noise Filtering on Inductive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02615)
*Carl M. Kadie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"有效维度"（effective dimension）这一新学习度量，用于将学习问题属性与平均情况下的学习性能联系起来，可经验估计并做出平均情况预测，在反向传播等系统上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习研究的核心目标之一是度量、建模并理解学习问题属性如何影响平均情况下的学习性能，例如量化构造性归纳、噪声过滤和背景知识的价值。已有的VC维度等度量与实际学习性能的联系存在局限，需要一种更通用、可经验估计且能预测平均性能的学习度量。

Method: 提出"有效维度"这一定义度量。它与VC维度类似，常与问题属性呈简单线性关系；但不同之处在于它可以通过经验方式估计，并能对平均情况作出预测，因而更广泛适用于机器学习和人类学习研究。作者在包括反向传播在内的多个学习系统上对该度量进行了演示，并用它预测使用特征构造系统FRINGE所带来的收益。

Result: 有效维度在多个学习系统（含反向传播）上得到成功演示；并能精确预测使用FRINGE特征构造系统的收益，且发现该收益随目标概念复杂度的增加而下降。

Conclusion: 有效维度是一种比VC维度更具适用性的学习度量，既能与问题属性保持简单的线性关系，又可经验估计并预测平均情况性能，适用于机器与人类学习研究，并可精确刻画特征构造等方法的实际收益及其随问题复杂度的变化规律。

Abstract: Learning research, as one of its central goals, tries to measure, model, and understand how learning-problem properties affect average-case learning performance. For example, we would like to quantify the value of constructive induction, noise filtering, and background knowledge. This paper describes the effective dimension, a new learning measure that helps link problem properties to learning performance. Like the Vapnik-Chervonenkis (VC) dimension, the effective dimension is often in a simple linear relation with problem properties. Unlike the VC dimension, the effective dimension can be estimated empirically and makes average-case predictions. It is therefore more widely applicable to machine and human learning research. The measure is demonstrated on several learning systems including Backpropagation. Finally, the measure is used to precisely predict the benefit of using FRINGE, a feature construction system. The benefit is found to decrease as the complexity of the target concept increases.

</details>


### [112] [Online Verification of Language Model Responses Under Cost Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02632)
*Erfan Hajihashemi,Yanning Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了OMVV算法，通过在线多验证器池和指数权重路由策略，在有限成本下验证大模型推理输出的正确性，兼顾准确率与成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型多步推理部署中，验证输出正确性至关重要。查询昂贵的真值oracle在每个在线步骤都不现实。已有方法用单个弱验证器每步打分来决定是否调用强验证器，但固定的单一弱验证器在主题或难度变化时表现不稳定，事先选定会导致成本过高或验证不准。

Method: OMVV维护一个包含K个候选弱验证器的池子，它们具有不同的成本和验证性能。每轮通过在线分数组合器（online score combiner）和指数权重路由策略，自适应地将决策路由到选定的验证器。

Result: OMVV提供了无分布假设的、有限时间的假接受率和假拒绝率保证，覆盖整个验证器池；并在联合成本与一致性目标下实现对最佳固定验证器的次线性遗憾（sublinear regret）。推理数据集基准实验表明，在多种操作预算下，OMVV比任何单一固定验证器都实现了更高准确率和更低验证成本。

Conclusion: 通过维护多个弱验证器池并在线自适应路由，OMVV在保证理论性能的同时，实验上优于单一固定验证器，兼顾了准确率与成本。

Abstract: As large language models are increasingly deployed for multi-step reasoning, verifying the correctness of their outputs has become essential for maintaining reliability at scale. Verifying the correctness of large language model outputs is often done by querying a costly ground-truth oracle, which is impractical to invoke at every step in an online setting. Prior work addresses this by querying a single weak verifier on every step, and using its score to decide whether the costly strong verifier needs to be queried as well, reserving strong verification for only a small fraction of the steps. However, a single fixed weak verifier may not perform consistently well as the subject matter or difficulty of incoming queries changes over time, and committing to one in advance risks either overly costly or inaccurate verification. We introduce OMVV (Online Multi-Verifier Verification), an algorithm that maintains a pool of $K$ candidate weak verifiers with differing cost and verification performance, and adaptively routes each round's decision to a verifier selected via an online score combiner and an exponential-weights routing policy. OMVV provides a distribution-free, finite-time guarantee on false-accept and false-reject rates across the full pool of verifiers, and further achieves sublinear regret against the best fixed verifier in hindsight under a combined cost and consistency objective. Experiments on reasoning dataset benchmarks show that OMVV achieves higher accuracy at lower verification cost than any single fixed verifier, across a range of operating budgets.

</details>


### [113] [Context-Tower Conversion Preserves Generation While Freezing Retains Knowledge: Low-Budget AR-to-Diffusion Conversion of MoE LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02657)
*Wentao Lu,Jesse Clark,Tianyu Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在相同协议与1B token预算下，将30B MoE自回归模型转换为扩散语言模型，"冻结塔+交叉注意力"方案在HumanEval pass@10上达71.60，远优于"原位更新"方案的6.19（提升11.6倍），并保留父模型95% GSM8K与99% MMLU-Pro性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将预训练自回归（AR）模型转换为扩散语言模型（dLLM）可实现并行生成而无需重新预训练，但现有各类转换方法在训练数据规模上相差约三个数量级，且从未在统一协议下比较，因此难以判断哪种转换方式更优。

Method: 以同一个30B MoE父模型为起点，固定语料、监督token预算、可训练参数集合与评测框架，在各自训练配方下比较两类转换：(1) 原位（in-place）模型用去噪损失与表征对齐损失更新父模型部分权重；(2) 冻结塔（frozen-tower）模型通过交叉注意力，条件化于父模型的一个冻结因果副本。另做稠密父模型实验复现HumanEval差距，并进行约500M token的双塔消融（冻结/训练上下文塔），并给出理论分析。

Result: 1B训练token下，冻结塔模型HumanEval pass@10为71.60，原位模型仅6.19，提升11.6倍；同预算下冻结塔保留父模型95%的GSM8K与99%的MMLU-Pro分数。稠密父模型实验重现了HumanEval上的分离。约500M token的双塔设计中，冻结上下文塔比训练它保留显著更多的MMLU-Pro性能，而两者观测到的HumanEval分数相近。理论分析表明：两类转换在硬注意力掩码与每轮从左到右承诺一个位置的设定下，都包含AR父模型的精确采样器；在共享损失下，冻结消除了经由上下文状态的梯度贡献。此外，评测协议会显著影响某已发表的500B token转换在各项任务上的分数（双向），而其AR父模型分数变化不到3分，说明比较dLLM需要统一协议。

Conclusion: 在所测试的低预算条件下，冻结塔配置比原位转换更能保留父模型的生成能力；同时，缺乏统一评测协议会严重扭曲dLLM之间的比较结论。

Abstract: Converting a pretrained autoregressive (AR) model to a diffusion language model (dLLM) enables parallel generation without pretraining a new model. Published conversion methods differ by roughly three orders of magnitude in training data and have not been compared under a common protocol. We compare two conversions of the same 30B Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) parent, holding the corpus, supervised-token budget, trainable parameter set and evaluation harness fixed, each under its own training recipe. The in-place model updates a subset of the parent's weights using denoising and representation-alignment losses; the frozen-tower model instead conditions through cross-attention on a frozen causal copy of the parent. With 1B training tokens, the frozen-tower model scores 71.60 on HumanEval pass@10 against 6.19 for the in-place model, an 11.6x improvement. At the same budget it also keeps 95% of the parent's GSM8K score and 99% of its MMLU-Pro score. A dense-parent experiment reproduces the HumanEval separation. Within the two-tower design at about 500M tokens, freezing the context tower retains substantially more MMLU-Pro performance than training it, while both give similar observed HumanEval scores. Our theoretical analysis establishes that both conversion classes contain an exact sampler for the AR parent under a hard attention mask and left-to-right commitment of one position per round. Under a shared loss, freezing removes the gradient contribution through the context states. Furthermore, evaluation protocol substantially affects a published 500B-token conversion's scores in both directions across tasks, while its AR parent's scores vary by less than three points, so comparing dLLMs needs a common protocol. These results show that, in the tested low-budget regime, the frozen-tower configuration retains substantially more of the parent's generation performance than in-place conversion.

</details>


### [114] [Distributed Learning with Selective State Space Models: Architecture-Aware Convergence Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02659)
*Adam Piaseczny,Md Kamran Chowdhury Shisher,Shiqiang Wang,Christopher G. Brinton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对现代选择性状态空间模型（如Mamba2）在联邦学习中的行为缺乏理论理解的问题，推导了架构感知的梯度与平滑性界，并据此解释联邦优化算法的实际表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代状态空间模型（SSM，如Mamba2）以线性时间建模兼具递归动态，是Transformer的有力替代。但其在分布式（联邦）学习中的行为尚不清楚，且现有联邦学习方法大多与架构无关，未考虑现代选择性SSM的稳定性、选择性和状态空间参数化特性。

Method: 推导单层与多层选择性SSM的架构感知梯度界和平滑性界，以及FedAvg和FedProx的收敛界，刻画递归稳定性、输入依赖离散化和状态投影范数对联邦优化的影响；在教师SSM生成的序列上数值验证单层界；并基于分析提出关于本地训练和客户端异构性的预期，通过对比9种联邦学习算法在Mamba2语言建模、6个文本领域上的表现进行检验。

Result: 数值实验验证了单层SSM的理论界；在Mamba2语言建模的6个文本领域上对比9种联邦学习算法，结果表明SSM特有的界可用于解释实际联邦学习算法的行为。

Conclusion: 架构感知的理论界为理解和解释选择性SSM在联邦学习中的实际表现提供了基础，弥合了架构无关的联邦学习理论与现代SSM特性之间的差距。

Abstract: Modern state space models (SSMs), such as Mamba2, provide a compelling alternative to transformers by combining linear-time sequence modeling with recurrent state-space dynamics. However, the behavior of SSMs in distributed learning settings remains poorly understood. In particular, the existing standard federated learning methods are largely architecture-agnostic, and do not account for the stability, selectivity, and state-space parameterization that characterize modern selective SSMs. To address this, we derive architecture-aware gradient and smoothness bounds for single- and multi-layer selective SSMs, and convergence bounds for FedAvg and FedProx, characterizing how recurrent stability, input-dependent discretization, and state projection norms affect federated optimization. We then numerically validate the single-layer bounds on sequences generated by a teacher SSM, using a learner that follows the analyzed recurrence. We use this analysis to formulate expectations about the effects of local training and client heterogeneity, and examine these expectations by comparing nine federated learning algorithms on Mamba2 language modeling across six text domains. These experiments illustrate how SSM-specific bounds can provide a basis for interpreting the behavior of practical federated learning algorithms.

</details>


### [115] [AIGS: Adaptive Incremental Gating System for Online Representation Learning in Non-Stationary Data Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02661)
*SiRui He,Kai Liang Lew,Chui Zi Ong,Chean Khim Toa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应增量门控系统（AIGS），一种轻量级闭环状态感知适应框架，通过“冲击比”残差反馈机制在稳定性与可塑性之间平滑调节，解决在线表征学习中的稳定性-可塑性困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WoT与边缘计算中的实时数据流常经历潜在的状态变化（概念漂移），现有方法采用固定更新策略或滚动窗口，导致突变时参数僵化、稳定时浪费计算资源。核心问题是解决稳定性-可塑性困境：既要保留有用历史知识，又要快速响应概念漂移。

Method: AIGS引入“冲击比（Shock Ratio）”，一种内生残差反馈机制，将当前重构误差对照近期变化进行归一化。该信号驱动“连续可塑性控制器”，在学习可塑性与记忆保留之间平滑插值，将表征学习视为闭环控制机制，从而避免灾难性遗忘并保持严格的线性每步复杂度O(k·d)，适合延迟敏感的边端设备。

Result: 在真实智慧城市动态数据流（交通网络、气象系统、工业基础设施）上实验：在ETTm1和ETTm2数据集上，渐进退化下实现8.31与9.88步的预防性预警提前量；在PMS交通数据集上，突变后恢复速度显著更快；在高噪声Weather数据集上，提升异常召回同时抵抗随机噪声过拟合。

Conclusion: AIGS是一种实用、即插即用的适配器，适用于资源受限的边缘监控系统。

Abstract: Real-time data streams in Web of Things (WoT) and edge computing environments often evolve through latent regime changes. For online representation learning under strict computational constraints, the central problem is resolving the stability-plasticity dilemma: keeping useful historical knowledge while rapidly reacting to concept drift. Existing methods employ fixed update schedules or rolling windows. However, they suffer from parameter ossification during sudden shifts and waste computational resources when the stream remains stable. This paper proposes the Adaptive Incremental Gating System (AIGS), a lightweight closed-loop state-aware adaptation framework. AIGS introduces the Shock Ratio, an endogenous residual feedback mechanism that normalizes current reconstruction error against recent variation. This signal drives a Continuous Plasticity Controller that smoothly interpolates between learning plasticity and memory retention. By treating representation learning as a closed-loop control mechanism, AIGS avoids catastrophic forgetting and maintains a strictly linear $\mathcal{O}\left(k\cdot d\right)$ per-step complexity suitable for latency-sensitive edge devices. Experiments on real-world smart city dynamic streams-spanning traffic networks, meteorological systems, and industrial infrastructure-demonstrate distinct domain-dependent advantages. On Electricity Transformer Temperature datasets, AIGS achieves preventative early-warning lead times of 8.31 (ETTm1) and 9.88 (ETTm2) steps under gradual degradation. On Performance Measurement System traffic datasets, it shows significantly faster post-shift recovery after abrupt mutations. On the highly noisy Weather dataset, it improves anomaly recall while resisting stochastic noise overfitting. These findings establish AIGS as a practical, plug-and-play adapter for resource-constrained edge monitoring systems.

</details>


### [116] [Mind the Refinement Gap: When Safe High-Level Robot Plans Produce Unsafe Executions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02662)
*Stabak Das,Priyesh Ranjan,Xiangfang Li,Lijun Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文审计了语言驱动机器人系统中"轨迹完备性"假设，即安全监控器检查的高层动作序列是否真实反映执行中的导航与隐式动作效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言使能的机器人系统常将语义图规划与时间逻辑安全监控器结合，但隐含假设了监控器所检查的高层动作序列足以代表执行中的真实轨迹效果，该假设存在潜在安全风险。

Method: 在RoboGuard中审计该假设，比较同一LTL规范下表面计划与图精炼轨迹的判定结果；评估包含28个受控案例（覆盖五类动作抽象族）及14个端到端案例（SPINE由自然语言生成计划，RoboGuard生成场景接地安全规范）。

Result: 受控评估中，全部12个目标抽象案例均出现预测的表面—精炼差异，而16个对照案例表现符合预期。

Conclusion: 结果支持将基于图的轨迹精炼作为轻量级缓解措施，并可作为物理AI安全监控器的诊断工具。

Abstract: Language-enabled robot systems increasingly combine semantic-graph planning with temporal-logic safety monitors. We investigate a trace-completeness assumption in these systems: whether the high-level action sequence checked by a monitor represents the navigation and implicit action effects induced during execution. We audit this assumption in RoboGuard by comparing its verdict on a surface plan with its verdict on a graph-refined trace under the same Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) specification. Our evaluation comprises 28 controlled cases spanning five action-abstraction families and 14 end-to-end cases in which SPINE [1] generates plans from natural-language instructions while RoboGuard generates scene-grounded safety specifications. In the controlled evaluation, all 12 targeted abstraction cases exhibit the predicted surface-versus-refined discrepancy while all 16 controls behave as expected, motivating graph-based trace refinement as a lightweight mitigation and a diagnostic tool for physical-AI safety monitors.

</details>


### [117] [LEAP: Learning Efficient Action Proposals For LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02670)
*Zhen Xu,Qizheng Zhang,Gerry Wan,Shang Zhu,Ce Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出动作推测延迟框架并基于此设计LEAP方法，用小草稿模型训练出高准确率，使智能体端到端提速最高60%且不损失成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体逐步执行任务，每一步需推理并选择动作，前一步未完成则无法开始下一步，导致rollout速度慢。现有动作阶段推测方法依赖现成模型：大草稿模型匹配率高但耗时长，小草稿模型快但几乎不与目标模型做相同决策。作者由此提出更一般的问题：动作推测的端到端加速究竟由什么决定。

Method: 作者建立了一个动作推测轮次（round）的延迟框架，比较一轮的收益与成本。收益取决于草稿模型对目标模型的预测准确度，以及任务在结束前可执行的步数；成本来自草稿生成、目标验证等待以及工具执行。在该框架指导下提出LEAP（Learning Efficient Action Proposals）：保持草稿模型小，但通过在目标动作序列上训练使其准确。使用0.6B小模型，LEAP在多数决策上与目标模型一致。此外，草稿模型可在线训练，无需事先收集轨迹。

Result: LEAP使智能体端到端墙钟时间最高快60%，任务成功率无系统性变化。在多种数据集、目标模型和草稿模型上，该框架能解释大部分实测加速。在线训练草稿模型（无需事先轨迹收集）可匹配离线训练的性能。

Conclusion: 动作推测的端到端加速由草稿准确度、任务剩余步数、草稿与验证及工具执行成本共同决定。训练小型专用草稿模型（LEAP）可在不损失成功率的前提下显著加速智能体，并且支持在线训练，便于实际部署。

Abstract: LLM agents are known to be slow in rollouts. An agent completes a task one step at a time. At each step, it reasons and then chooses an action to execute. The next step and action cannot start until the previous one has finished. Speculative decoding accelerates the rollouts at the reason phase by drafting and verifying the inference tokens. Recent works have also started to apply similar ideas at the action phase. These works use off-the-shelf models, usually large, to draft action proposals for target model to verify. Large drafters match the target more often but take longer to propose, while small off-the-shelf models are fast but rarely make the same decision as the target. We ask a more general question: what determines the end-to-end speedup of action speculation? To answer it, we develop a latency framework for the speculative round. The framework compares what a round gains with what it costs. The gain depends on how well the drafter predicts the target and on how many steps the task can take before it ends. The cost comes from drafting, from waiting for target verification and from executing tools. Guided by the framework, we introduce LEAP (Learning Efficient Action Proposals) which keeps the drafter small and makes it accurate by training it on the target actions sequences. With a small 0.6B model, LEAP agrees with the target on most decisions and makes agents up to 60% faster in end-to-end wall clock time, with no systematic change in task success. Across various datasets, target models and draft models, the framework accounts for most of the measured speedups. We also show the draft model can be online trained with no prior trace collection and match the performance of offline training, making LEAP practical to deploy in the real world.

</details>


### [118] [RAOA: Alternating-Operator Neural Computation with Programmable Radio Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02683)
*Toshiaki Koike-Akino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出RAOA（Radio Alternating Operator Ansatz），一种交替执行"问题更新"与"混合更新"的循环计算架构，探索可编程无线电传播能否作为计算深度而非仅通信通道，并通过离散优化、可编程传播仿真和预训练模型适配三个场景验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可编程无线电传播多被视为通信通道或一次性模拟变换，作者希望探究其能否承担"计算深度"的角色。同时尝试将交替算子计算、可编程射频传播与神经网络适配统一到同一循环框架中，以理解重复计算能否在不增加学习控制参数的情况下提升性能。

Method: 提出RAOA循环架构：在持久隐状态上交替执行能量导出的"问题更新"与"混合更新"，每次混合后重算问题场，使重复传递具有组合性，即使跨深度复用相同的学习控制也能奏效。通过三条路径评估：(1) 精确离散优化；(2) 受约束的可编程传播仿真（无源、仅相位、自由空间模型）；(3) 作为零初始化残差适配器插入预训练语言模型。

Result: 在离散目标上，重复执行可提升解质量而不增加学习控制数量，且同一形式可直接处理高阶交互；无源相位型自由空间模型表明所需算子可由可编程传播近似，虽有实现误差但保留有用的下游行为。作为零初始化残差适配器时，RAOA在三个模型族上对WikiText的适配性能接近同规模浅层MLP，但推理任务迁移依赖具体模型。

Conclusion: 该工作将交替算子计算、可编程射频传播与神经适配统一在同一循环框架中，表明无线电传播有潜力作为计算深度使用。但需注意，射频实现证据基于仿真而非硬件演示，实际可行性仍待验证。

Abstract: Can programmable radio propagation serve as computational depth rather than only as a communication channel or one-shot analog transform? We introduce the Radio Alternating Operator Ansatz (RAOA), a recurrent computing architecture that alternates an energy-derived problem update with a mixing update over a persistent latent state. Recomputing the problem field after each mix makes repeated passes compositional even when the same learned controls are reused across depth. We evaluate this idea through exact discrete optimization, constrained programmable-propagation simulation, and pretrained-model adaptation. On discrete objectives, repeated execution can improve solution quality without increasing the learned-control count, and the same formulation handles higher-order interactions directly. A passive phase-only free-space model further shows that the required operators can be approximated by programmable propagation while retaining useful downstream behavior despite realization error. When inserted as a zero-initialized residual adapter, RAOA adapts pretrained language models with WikiText performance close to a matched shallow MLP across three model families, while reasoning-task transfer remains model-dependent. Together, these results connect alternating-operator computation, programmable radio propagation, and neural adaptation within one recurrent framework. The RF realization evidence is simulation-based rather than a hardware demonstration.

</details>


### [119] [Test-time Calibration Learning for Large Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02695)
*Zizhuo Zhang,Xiong Peng,Jingwei Sun,Rong Yao,Shixiong Kai,Mingxuan Yuan,Bo Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出无标签的测试时校准学习框架TTCL，在无真值标签的目标任务数据上联合自适应推理准确率与言语化置信度，显著提升准确率并降低校准误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的大语言模型不仅需要准确答案，还需置信度真实反映正确概率。现有将校准学习引入强化学习的方法依赖真值标签监督，在测试时无标签、需适应新目标任务的实际场景中受限。

Method: 提出TTCL框架，在无标签目标数据上直接从多个模型生成的回答中提取准确性与校准的自监督信号，实现无需真值的测试时校准学习；并从理论上证明TTCL是理想校准目标的有界替代。

Result: 在数学推理与事实问答任务上，TTCL在多种模型与任务中一致提升准确率与校准。基座模型在八个基准上平均相对准确率提升+40.13%，ECE降低+70.80%；math-to-factQA设置下平均相对准确率增益+20.35%，ECE降低+53.83%。

Conclusion: TTCL为无标签测试时场景提供了有效的校准学习方案，可进一步改善已校准模型在域偏移下的表现，尤其当源域校准难以迁移到目标任务时。

Abstract: Reliable large language models (LLMs) must not only produce accurate answers but also express confidence that faithfully reflects their probability of being correct. Such calibration is essential for identifying uncertain predictions and supporting reliable decision-making in real-world deployment. Recent studies incorporate calibration learning into reinforcement learning (RL), jointly optimizing answer correctness and verbalized confidence using ground-truth correctness supervision. However, their reliance on labeled data limits their applicability in practical test-time settings, where ground-truth labels are unavailable and calibration may need to adapt to newly encountered target tasks. To address this challenge, we propose Test-Time Calibration Learning (TTCL), a label-free framework that jointly adapts reasoning accuracy and verbalized confidence directly on unlabeled target-task data. Specifically, TTCL derives self-supervision signals for both correctness and calibration from multiple model-generated responses, enabling calibration learning at test time without ground-truth labels. Theoretical analysis further establishes TTCL as a bounded surrogate for the ideal calibration objective. Extensive experiments on mathematical reasoning and factual question answering demonstrate that TTCL consistently improves both accuracy and calibration across diverse models and tasks. On base models, TTCL achieves an average relative accuracy improvement of +40.13% and an ECE reduction of +70.80% across eight benchmarks. Moreover, TTCL can further improve both accuracy and calibration for already calibrated models under domain shift, particularly when source-domain calibration transfers poorly to target tasks. In the math-to-factQA setting, TTCL achieves an average relative accuracy gain of +20.35% and reduces ECE by +53.83%. The source code is released at https://github.com/tmlr-group/TTCL.

</details>


### [120] [Learning from Evolving Errors: Adaptive Iterative Repair for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02700)
*Rui Li,Liyang He,Zheng Zhang,Zhenya Huang,Linbo Zhu,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AIR-OPD，一种面向on-policy蒸馏的自适应迭代修复框架，通过生成错误到修复的引导信号，避免学生仅依赖参考解导致的捷径问题，并在多个数学推理基准上取得领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: on-policy自蒸馏（OPSD）相比强化学习提供更密集的token级反馈，但其教师以学生无法获得的完整参考解为条件，导致“解条件捷径”风险：参考解只指定目标，却不给出如何从当前错误走向目标的路径。因此需要一种提供错误到修复监督的方法。

Method: AIR-OPD为自适应迭代修复框架：给定失败回答，引导生成器为当前错误合成修复引导；学生据此进行on-policy重试；若仍错误，则针对新错误生成新引导。每轮中固定教师以引导为特权上下文，对学生最新失败回答的错误对齐区域进行监督。并采用结果感知的阶段加权，偏向早期修复阶段，并奖励即时重试通过验证的阶段。

Result: 在DAPO-Math-17K上训练，AIME24、AIME25、HMMT25及分布外MMLU-Pro、GPQA上评估。考察自引导（当前学生策略）与外部引导（更大模型）两种来源。对Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B，AIR-OPD均取得最佳数学推理平均成绩，较最强基线提升至多3.6分，同时在分布外基准上保持基座模型性能。

Conclusion: AIR-OPD通过迭代错误修复引导缓解了解条件捷径问题，在数学推理任务上稳定优于现有基线，并具备良好的分布外性能保持能力。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) supplies dense token-level feedback on trajectories sampled from the student's own policy, a richer training signal than the outcome-level rewards of reinforcement learning. This feedback comes from a teacher conditioned on a full reference solution unavailable to the student. The reference solution specifies the target but not how to move from the student's current error toward it, creating a solution-conditioned shortcut risk. We introduce AIR-OPD, an adaptive iterative repair framework for on-policy distillation that provides error-to-repair supervision. Given a failed response, a guidance generator synthesizes repair guidance for the current error. The student samples an on-policy retry with this guidance. If the retry remains incorrect, the generator produces new repair guidance for the newly observed error. At each round, a fixed teacher receives the guidance as privileged context and supervises the student on an error-aligned region of its latest failed response. Outcome-aware stage weighting favors early repair stages and credits stages whose immediate retry passes verification. We train AIR-OPD on the DAPO-Math-17K dataset and evaluate on AIME24, AIME25, and HMMT25, alongside out-of-distribution tests on MMLU-Pro and GPQA. We examine two guidance sources, self-guidance from the current student policy and external guidance from a larger model. For both Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B, AIR-OPD attains the best mathematical-reasoning averages, improving over the strongest baseline by up to 3.6 points, while preserving base-model performance on the out-of-distribution benchmarks.

</details>


### [121] [Conditional Capacity and Routing in Mixture-of-Experts Particle Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02701)
*Kaushik Pendiyala,Haris Zia,Trevin Lee,Timothy Legge,Alejandro J. De Leon,Zihan Zhao,Aaron Wang,Abhijith Gandrakota,Jennifer Ngadiuba,Richard Cavanaugh,Javier Duarte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在188类JetClass-II上系统比较稠密与MoE粒子Transformer，发现避免token丢弃时top-1 MoE可在几乎不增加标称前向计算的情况下超越稠密基线，但增加存储专家数量收益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型能提升参数容量而不成比例增加激活计算，但这一权衡在粒子物理Transformer中如何表现尚不明确，需要厘清存储参数容量、激活计算、路由容量与路由组织对喷注分类的影响。

Method: 在188类JetClass-II数据集上研究稠密与MoE粒子Transformer，变化专家数量、路由容量、top-K和辅助损失等设置，并进行路由分析，考察专家分配与粒子身份及运动学特征的关联。

Result: 避免token丢弃时，top-1 MoE在几乎不变的标称前向计算下优于稠密基线；进一步增加存储专家数量带来的精度提升很小；每token激活多个专家可提升预测性能但计算成本更高。路由分析显示，某些配置下专家分配与粒子身份和运动学关联更强，但这种结构与分类性能并非单调相关。

Conclusion: 评估用于喷注分类的稀疏专家模型时，需要区分存储参数容量、激活计算、路由容量与路由组织这几个维度。代码与实验配置已公开。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models can increase parameter capacity without proportionally increasing active computation, but it is unclear how this trade-off behaves in particle-physics transformers. We study dense and MoE Particle Transformers on 188-class JetClass-II, varying expert count, routing capacity, top-K, and auxiliary loss. We find that, when token dropping is avoided, top-1 MoE models improve over the dense baseline at nearly unchanged nominal forward compute, while further increasing the number of stored experts produces little additional accuracy gain. Activating multiple experts per token yields additional predictive improvements at higher computational cost. Routing analyses show that expert assignments become more strongly associated with particle identity and kinematics in some configurations, but this structure does not increase monotonically with classification performance. These results highlight the need to distinguish stored parameter capacity, active computation, routing capacity, and routing organization when evaluating sparse expert models for jet classification. Code and experiment configurations are available at https://github.com/kpendiyala/MPT.

</details>


### [122] [MuonIO: Principled Norm-Aware Descent for Embedding Tables and Language Model Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02705)
*Linkai Ma,Xinyu Luo,Mengbo Wang,Ananth Grama,Petros Drineas,Brian Bullins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MuonIO 将 Muon 风格的归一化更新规则统一应用于语言模型的输入嵌入层和输出头，基于算子范数（2→�[infinity](/infinity) 与 1→2）论证，显著减少优化器状态内存与更新 FLOPs 并提升困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 Muon 只对隐藏线性层使用其原理化的谱范数更新规则，却把输入嵌入表和输出语言模型头排除在外，改用 AdamW。这种分离缺乏理论一致性：嵌入接收 one-hot 输入，头输出经 softmax 且具有 Lipschitz 几何。论文希望为这两层也提供原理化的、统一的 Muon 式更新，同时降低其优化器状态与计算开销。

Method: 针对语言模型头 L∈R^{V×d}，基于 softmax 输出的 Lipschitz 连续性，采用 2→∞ 算子范数；针对嵌入表 E∈R^{d×V}，基于 one-hot 输入几何，采用 1→2 算子范数。利用恒等式 ‖L‖_{2→∞}=‖L^⊤‖_{1→2}，将两者纳入同一词表导向几何：MuonIO 应用单一归一化向量规则——对 E 作列归一化，对 L 作行归一化。

Result: 在 1B LLaMA、C4 预训练上，MuonIO 相比 Muon 将输入/输出优化器状态内存减少 50%、I/O 更新 FLOPs 减少约 46%，同时改善了验证困惑度。

Conclusion: MuonIO 用一个统一的、理论驱动的 Muon 风格规则替代了此前对嵌入与输出头使用 AdamW 的做法，在保持或提升语言建模性能的同时大幅节省内存与计算，说明把输入输出层纳入 Muon 框架是可行且有效的。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer derives its update rule for hidden linear layers by solving a local linearization of the loss penalized by the spectral norm, motivated by an RMS-stability argument for dense linear layers. Standard Muon implementations, however, exclude the input (embedding table) and output (language model head) layers from this principled treatment, for which they use AdamW instead. We present MuonIO, a single Muon-style update for both of these layers. For the language model head $\mathbf{L} \in \mathbb{R}^{V \times d}$, we motivate the use of the $2\to\infty$ operator norm, due to the Lipschitz continuity of the softmax output geometry, while for the embedding table $\mathbf{E} \in \mathbb{R}^{d \times V}$, we draw on the $1 \to 2$ operator norm, based on the one-hot input geometry identified by Bernstein & Newhouse (2025). The identity $\lVert\mathbf{L}\rVert_{2\to\infty}=\lVert\mathbf{L}^\top\rVert_{1\to2}$ then puts both matrices in the same vocabulary-oriented geometry: MuonIO applies a single normalized-vector rule, which appears as column normalization for $\mathbf{E}$ and row normalization for $\mathbf{L}$. Empirical evaluations demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, with MuonIO reducing I/O optimizer state memory by 50% and I/O update FLOPs by $\sim$46% compared to Muon for 1B LLaMA pretraining on C4, while also improving validation perplexity.

</details>


### [123] [Differential Privacy of Gradient Descent on Perturbed Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02716)
*Austin Watkins,Raman Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了目标扰动（objective perturbation）在有限次梯度下降迭代下的隐私与效用保证：对强凸光滑且Hessian Lipschitz的目标，证明扰动到第N次迭代的映射在隐私论证所用有界域上是C^1微分同胚，并给出Jacobian最小奇异值的定量下界，从而可对有限迭代做直接换元分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统目标扰动释放的是精确扰动最小化器，但实际只能通过有限步优化近似得到，因此需要分析有限迭代输出的隐私与效用，填补精确解与有限计算之间的理论空白。

Method: 对正则化经验风险加随机线性项〈z,w〉（z一次性采样自N(0,σ²I_d)），释放确定性梯度下降第N次迭代。在强凸、光滑、Hessian Lipschitz条件下，证明z↦w_N为C^1微分同胚并给出Jacobian最小奇异值下界，进而用换元法分析有限迭代的隐私轮廓，并让截断参数随N缓慢增长以逼近精确解证书。

Result: 对广义线性模型，迭代条件满足后隐私轮廓界不含显式环境维度因子，且有限迭代修正项几何衰减；随截断参数增长可恢复精确最小化器证书。期望超额经验风险被dσ²/(2μ)加上几何衰减的优化项所界定，并可迁移到总体风险，且主要统计项无需额外的条件数因子。

Conclusion: 有限步梯度下降的目标扰动可达到与精确最小化器相当（极限意义下）的隐私与效用保证，隐私界摆脱显式维度依赖，效用界由统计项加快速衰减的优化误差构成。

Abstract: Objective perturbation adds a random linear term to a regularized empirical risk and releases the exact perturbed minimizer. We study the finite computation obtained by releasing the $N$-th iterate of deterministic gradient descent on $w\mapsto F(w;S)+\langle z,w\rangle$, where $z\sim\mathcal N(0,σ^2I_d)$ is drawn once before optimization. For strongly convex and smooth objectives with Lipschitz Hessian, we prove an explicit condition under which the map $z\mapsto w_N$ is a $C^1$-diffeomorphism on the bounded domains used in the privacy argument, with a quantitative lower bound on the smallest singular value of its Jacobian. This permits a direct change-of-variables analysis of the finite iterate. For generalized linear models, the resulting privacy-profile bound has no explicit ambient-dimension factor once the iteration condition holds, and its finite-iteration correction decreases geometrically. By letting the free truncation parameter grow slowly with $N$, we recover the corresponding exact-minimizer certificate in the limit. We also bound the expected excess empirical risk by $dσ^2/(2μ)$ plus a geometrically decreasing optimization term, and transfer the result to population risk without an additional multiplicative condition-number factor in the leading statistical terms.

</details>


### [124] [Structural-Functional Brain Connectivity Generation via Multimodal Hypergraph-based Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02722)
*Chyong Yi Poh,Hwa Hui Tew,Junn Yong Loo,Raphaël C. -W. Phan,Fuad Noman,Pew-Thian Yap,Chee-Ming Ting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MHG-FM框架，用超图神经网络和流匹配联合生成并互译脑结构-功能连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构连接(SC)与功能连接(FC)提供互补的脑区交互信息，但大规模配对SC-FC数据稀缺。现有生成方法多用成对图、仅捕捉二元交互，且独立生成SC与FC，难以保留高阶结构-功能关系。

Method: 构建模态特异性超图，用超图神经网络(HGNN)编码器学习高阶表示，并通过双重交叉注意力(DCA)进行双向跨模态融合；用变分自编码器将融合表示映射到紧凑潜空间，在潜空间中以条件流匹配实现连接生成与多模态翻译。

Result: 在HCP-YA数据集上，MHG-FM在重建质量、拓扑保持、分布相似性和SC-FC耦合方面优于多个先进基线，采样速度比匹配的扩散骨干快约8倍。

Conclusion: 超图建模结合流匹配可有效捕捉高阶结构与功能关系，实现高质量的联合生成与跨模态翻译，并显著提升采样效率。

Abstract: Structural connectivity (SC) and functional connectivity (FC) provide complementary information on interactions between brain regions and are widely used in neuroimaging studies of neuropsychiatric disorders. Generative modelling can alleviate the scarcity of large-scale paired SC-FC data, but existing approaches typically use pairwise graphs that capture only dyadic interactions and often generate SC and FC independently, limiting preservation of higher-order structure-function relationships. We propose a Multimodal Hypergraph Flow Matching (MHG-FM) framework for joint SC-FC connectivity generation and cross-modal translation. MHG-FM constructs modality-specific hypergraphs, learns higher-order representations with Hypergraph Neural Network (HGNN) encoders, and performs bidirectional cross-modal fusion using Dual Cross-Attention (DCA). A variational autoencoder maps the fused representations to a compact latent space, where conditional flow matching enables connectivity synthesis and multimodal translation via latent transport. Experiments on the Human Connectome Project Young Adult (HCP-YA) dataset show that MHG-FM outperforms several state-of-the-art baselines in reconstruction quality, topology preservation, distributional similarity, and SC-FC coupling, while achieving approximately 8x faster sampling than a matched diffusion backbone.

</details>


### [125] [Bellman Error Minimization Via Linear Programming Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02730)
*Haining Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的函数逼近方法，用于降低高维动态规划与强化学习问题中的Bellman误差，并以收益管理中的网络容量控制问题为例，将深度神经网络与线性规划逼近算法相结合求解动态规划问题，仿真结果显示所提算法与基准方法相比具有竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维动态规划与强化学习问题中普遍存在Bellman误差较大的难题，传统方法在维数较高时难以有效求解，因此需要新的函数逼近途径来降低误差并提升近似解质量。

Method: 提出一种新的函数逼近方法；以收益管理中的网络容量控制这一经典动态规划问题作为动机示例，将深度神经网络与线性规划逼近算法相结合，从而求得动态规划问题的近似解。

Result: 仿真实验表明，所提出的逼近算法在与基准方法比较时取得了具有竞争力的性能表现。

Conclusion: 深度神经网络与线性规划逼近的结合能够有效用于求解动态规划问题，所提函数逼近方法在降低Bellman误差、处理高维问题方面具有潜力，并具备与现有基准方法相竞争的应用价值。

Abstract: This paper proposes a new functional approximation approach to reduce Bellman error in high-dimensional dynamic programming and Reinforcement Learning problems. Using a classic dynamic programming problem (network capacity control in revenue management) as the motivational example, the paper illustrates that deep neural networks and linear programming approximation algorithms can be combined to derive approximate solutions to dynamic programming problems. Simulation results show the proposed approximation algorithms achieves competitive performance when compared with benchmark.

</details>


### [126] [Inner Momentum for Differentially Private Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02738)
*Bishnu Bhusal,Minh Vu,Ben Southworth,Geigh Zollicoffer,Rohit Chadha,Manish Bhattarai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DP-Muon-IM，通过对模型历史平均后再裁剪，抑制差分隐私裁剪对Muon更新奇异向量几何的扰动，在私有GPT-2微调中提升BLEU与ROUGE-L。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私训练对每个样本梯度做径向裁剪，不同裁剪因子会扭曲平均梯度的相对奇异向量几何；而Muon更新近似为极因子UV^T，仅依赖奇异向量，因此对裁剪扰动尤为敏感，亟需缓解这一退化。

Method: 在裁剪前，将每个采样样本的Muon梯度在当前模型与近期模型历史短窗口上求平均；裁剪后的批矩阵可分解为公共缩放与协方差残差R，且||R||_F <= sigma_lambda sigma_G，直接界定裁剪引起的畸变；并证明在相应谱条件下有限步Newton-Schulz迭代保持输入的极因子，从而保证校正经正交化后仍然有效。

Result: 在E2E与DART上以epsilon∈{1,2,4,8}进行私有GPT-2微调时，DP-Muon-IM在每个同种子对比中均优于DP-Muon的BLEU与ROUGE-L；非私有诊断显示加噪前极误差降低2-4%。

Conclusion: 对梯度做模型历史平均再进行裁剪，可有效抑制裁剪对Muon奇异向量几何的破坏，理论与实验均支持该校正能通过正交化保留，提升差分隐私训练下的下游生成质量。

Abstract: Differentially private training clips each per-example gradient before adding noise. This clipping is radial for each example, yet unequal clipping factors can distort the relative singular-vector geometry of their average. Muon is particularly exposed to this effect, since its update is an approximate polar factor UV^T that depends only on the singular vectors that clipping can shift. To curb this degradation, we propose averaging each sampled example's Muon gradient over the current model and a short history of recent models before clipping. The clipped batch matrix then separates into a common rescaling and a covariance residual R between sampled gradients and clipping values, with ||R||_F <= sigma_lambda sigma_G, bounding the clipping-induced distortion directly. We further show that a finite Newton-Schulz iteration preserves the polar factor of its input under these spectral conditions, confirming that our correction survives orthogonalization. In private GPT-2 fine-tuning on E2E and DART at epsilon in {1, 2, 4, 8}, DP-Muon-IM improves BLEU and ROUGE-L over DP-Muon in every seed-matched comparison, and non-private diagnostics show 2-4% lower pre-noise polar error.

</details>


### [127] [Prospective Hindsight: Self-Calibrating Reinforcement Learning via Prediction-Reality Gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02740)
*Jiaxin Zhang,Xiangyu Peng,Qinglin Chen,Yu Li,Hiroaki Hayashi,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Prospective Hindsight (PH) 训练原则，利用 agent 行动前的预期预测与行动后回顾评估之间的"惊讶度"来加权梯度，使校准能力随优化过程自然涌现，在单轮与多轮任务上同时提升性能与校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习对长时程 agent 仅依赖纯回顾性训练信号：只有当观察到环境反馈后才分配信用，导致 agent 在行动时刻的"信念"无法进入梯度。这种事后信用分配忽略了对自身预测准确性的建模，造成校准（calibration）缺失问题未被直接优化。

Method: PH 在任意回顾式基方法上增加一个信号，来源是 agent 的预期预测（反馈前）与回顾评估（反馈后）之间的差距（惊喜度/surprise）。通过 stop-gradient 的惊喜度加权优势函数，放大自模型最不准确样本的梯度贡献。由于预期预测器与策略共享参数，二者协同演化，焦点逐步转向剩余盲区。作者进一步将该原则与特权信息差距相联系，证明最小化惊喜残差提供了降低误校准率的下行路径，故校准成为优化的副产物而非额外目标。

Result: 在单轮可验证任务与多轮个人 agent 任务上（基于 GRPO、on-policy distillation 及其组合），PH 同时提升任务性能与校准，并在不同模型规模下获得一致增益。值得注意的是，主导的误校准模式在不同场景间发生结构性转变——单轮为过度自信失败，多轮为欠自信成功——但同一训练原则均能成功应对。

Conclusion: PH 将"预期—回顾"差距作为自校准训练信号，无需额外校准目标即可让校准能力从优化中自然涌现，并能跨任务类型与模型规模稳健提升性能与校准，表明该原则具有通用性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for long-horizon agents relies on purely retrospective training signals: credit is assigned only after observing environmental consequences, leaving the agent's belief at action time invisible to the gradient. We introduce Prospective Hindsight (PH), a self-calibrating training principle that augments any retrospective base method with a signal derived from the gap between the agent's prospective prediction (before feedback) and the retrospective evaluation (after feedback). This per-rollout surprise identifies samples where the agent's self-model is most inaccurate and amplifies their gradient contribution through a stop-gradient surprise-weighted advantage. Since the prospective predictor shares parameters with the policy, the two co-evolve, progressively shifting focus to the agent's remaining blind spots. We connect this principle to a privileged-information gap and show that minimizing the surprise residual provides a descent pathway on the agent's miscalibration rate; calibration thus emerges as a byproduct of optimization rather than from an added objective. On single-turn verifiable tasks and a multi-turn personal-agent task (under GRPO, on-policy distillation, and their combination), PH improves both task performance and calibration, with consistent gains across model scales. Notably, the dominant miscalibration mode shifts structurally between regimes, overconfident failures in single-turn, underconfident successes in multi-turn, yet the same training principle addresses both successfully.

</details>


### [128] [Jumping up and down: Denoiser diffusion models for discrete ordinal data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02754)
*Yair Shenfeld,Ricardo Baptista,Stefano Peluchetti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出JUD——首个面向离散序数数据的去噪器式扩散模型族，支持双向扰动，在多种模态上取得有竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续空间（图像、视频）的扩散模型已高度成熟，离散类别数据（语言）的扩散模型近期也取得重大进展，但针对离散整数值（序数）数据的扩散模型发展不足，而这类数据在图像、音乐、基因计数等领域广泛存在。

Method: 提出Jumping Up and Down（JUD），一类新的基于去噪器的离散序数数据扩散模型。其核心是以训练去噪器为中心，同时允许对数据进行双向（上、下）扰动。训练目标简洁，扰动方式灵活。

Result: 得益于简洁的训练目标与双向扰动的灵活性，JUD在不同数据模态上均取得了有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: JUD填补了离散序数数据扩散建模的空白，作为首个以去噪器为核心、支持双向扰动的序数扩散模型族，展现出跨模态的通用性与竞争力。

Abstract: Diffusion models are highly developed in continuous spaces for image and video domains. Recently, major advances have been made for discrete diffusion models for categorical data, specifically in the language domain. In contrast, diffusion models for discrete integer-valued data are less developed, despite the prevalence of this modality, ranging from images and music to gene counts. We introduce Jumping Up and Down (JUD)---a new family of denoiser-based diffusion models for discrete ordinal data. This is the first family of diffusion models for ordinal data which centers around training denoisers, which at the same time allows for bi-directional (up and down) perturbations of the data. The simplicity of the training objective, combined with the flexibility of bi-directional perturbations, leads us to obtain competitive results across different data modalities.

</details>


### [129] [A Two-Stage Cascade for Near-Real-Time Forest Anomaly Detection from Sentinel-1 SAR Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02763)
*Pann Thinzar Seint,Subas Chhatkuli,Bryan Atwood*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段“统计-编码器”级联的近实时热带森林扰动异常检测框架，融合稳健统计z检验与卷积自编码器潜空间结构相似度确认，输出可审计的置信度与面积估计，服务MRV等工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热带森林监测对全球气候稳定与生物多样性保护至关重要，非法采伐、供应链透明度、土地利用治理与碳市场标准都亟需快速可靠的森林损失检测以实现及时干预。现有遥感监测面临两大瓶颈：光学监测受云覆盖限制，SAR系统则因季节性后向散射变化而把自然水分变化误判为森林损失。

Method: 构建两阶段级联架构，集成两个分析引擎：第一阶段对配准后的Sentinel-1 VH后向散射、同季节历史基线进行自适应稳健统计z分数检验；第二阶段为基于稳定森林斑块训练的卷积自编码器潜空间结构相似度（SSIM）的学习型确认门。仅当两阶段一致同意时才确认候选扰动为告警。

Result: 每条告警被赋予置信度分数，并依据重复发生历史划分为低/中/高三级风险等级；同时输出逐告警的可审计置信度分数与公顷面积估计，可直接对接监测、报告与核查（MRV）工作流、可持续林业管理、实地核查与环境风险评估。

Conclusion: 该级联方案在克服云覆盖限制与季节性后向散射干扰方面具备高保真检测能力，且模型灵活性支持从选择性采伐到大范围农业侵占制图、洪水制图等多种环境应用扩展。

Abstract: Tropical forest monitoring is essential for global climate stability and biodiversity preservation. To address the urgent need for rapid, reliable detection of forest loss which is essential for timely intervention against illegal logging, supply chain transparency, land-use governance and carbon market standards, we introduce a two-stage statistics-encoder cascade for near-real-time anomaly detection using Sentinel-1 time series. Our system is designed to overcome two fundamental challenges in remote sensing: the cloud-cover limitations that restrict optical monitoring and seasonal backscatter variation that causes SAR systems to mistake natural moisture changes for forest loss. The architecture integrates two distinct analytical engines to ensure high-fidelity detection: (1) an adaptive, robust-statistics z-score test on co-registered Sentinel-1 VH backscatter, same-season historical baseline and (2) a learned confirmation gate based on the latent-space structural similarity (SSIM) of a convolutional autoencoder trained on stable-forest patches. A candidate disturbance is confirmed as an alert only when both stages agree, and is assigned a confidence score and a Low/Medium/High risk tier from its repeat-occurrence history. The system produces per-alert auditable confidence scores and area-in-hectares estimates directly compatible with Monitoring, Reporting and Verification (MRV) workflows, sustainable forestry management, operational field checks and environmental risk assessments. Beyond its primary application, the model's flexibility allows for critical environmental applications ranging from selective logging to large-scale agricultural encroachment mapping, flood mapping and so on.

</details>


### [130] [A Controlled Audit of Personal AI Memory for Rating Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02764)
*Shivam Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过置换用户历史评分（保留评分分布、物品支持度和元数据）的审计方法，检验结构化评分预测中个人AI究竟依赖历史物品-评分关联还是用户评分倾向，并在Coat和MovieLens上对400个用户画像、6160个目标评分进行冻结评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在结构化评分预测中，个人AI到底是利用历史物品-评分关联，还是主要依赖用户自身的评分倾向，这一区分尚不清楚。澄清这一问题对于理解记忆提取、关联使用、输出可靠性与读取器选择各自的作用至关重要。

Method: 对每个用户的历史评分进行置换，同时严格保留评分分布、物品支持度与元数据；结合完整历史、原生记忆提取以及匹配的数值读取器，在Coat和MovieLens上对400个留出用户画像与6160个目标评分进行公开冻结评测；使用家庭调整的bootstrap区间与多种读取器（Qwen、Phi、history-only ridge）进行十项主要对比，并设固定回退规则保留全部2800次读取调用（含150次无效输出），另用独立实现核验输入、指标与对比结果。

Result: 在Coat上，Qwen编写的Mem0管线相对于完整历史使user-macro MAE分别上升0.084（Qwen）和 0.149（Phi），两个家庭调整bootstrap区间均排除零；正确的历史赋值对两个读取器在Coat上均有帮助，但MovieLens上的相应效应较小且调整后不具结论性。history-only ridge读取器在两个域上均优于Qwen，在MovieLens上优于Phi，而Coat上Phi的对比尚无定论。

Conclusion: 本研究是一项可复现的诊断性研究，表明记忆提取、关联使用、输出可靠性与读取器选择需要分别评估，而不能混为一谈。

Abstract: In structured rating prediction, does a personal AI use historical item-rating associations, or mainly the user's rating tendencies? We audit this distinction by permuting historical ratings within each user while preserving the exact rating distribution, item support, and metadata. We combine this control with full history, native memory extraction, and matched numerical readers in a publicly frozen evaluation of 400 held-out user profiles and 6,160 target ratings across Coat and MovieLens. On Coat, the tested Qwen-written Mem0 pipeline increases user-macro mean absolute error relative to full history by 0.084 for Qwen and 0.149 for Phi; both family-adjusted bootstrap intervals exclude zero. Correct historical assignments help both readers on Coat, but the corresponding MovieLens effects are smaller and inconclusive after adjustment. A history-only ridge reader outperforms Qwen in both domains and Phi on MovieLens, while the Coat Phi comparison is unresolved. All 2,800 reader calls, including 150 invalid outputs, are retained under a fixed fallback rule. A separate implementation verifies inputs, metrics, and all ten primary contrasts. The contribution is a reproducible diagnostic study showing why extraction, association use, output reliability, and reader choice require separate evaluation.

</details>


### [131] [Exact Memory-Time Optimization for Prefix-Cached Language Model Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02766)
*Shivam Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Prefix-Certificate Retention (PCR)，一种面向静态、分组、访问重置超时的精确有限轨迹前缀状态保留建模方法，将最大权重闭包化为最小割问题，并给出拓展到任意非负超时的断点定理与有序超时的网格 DP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型前缀状态缓存需在重计算与存储时间之间权衡。独立优化每个缓存块会高估收益，因为一个驻留块只有在所需前序前缀也可用时才可用。因此需要一个能精确刻画前缀依赖与超时交互的优化模型与可审计基准。

Method: 将可用前缀收益建模为节点，其前置条件包括超时阈值与前面的命中证书；把最大权重闭包归约为单次最小割，图规模与块查询次数和超时选择数成线性关系。用断点定理把构造扩展到所有非负超时且无离散化误差。针对有序超时推导网格规模线性的动态规划，并给出衡量该限制代价的界。用穷举小实例与 39,632 条公开 Mooncake 请求的时间顺序回放进行验证。

Result: 在固定网格上，有序超时在 120 个轨迹-分组-价格案例中有 118 个达到无限制训练最优。异构保留改善了若干留出集上的内存-时间权衡，但更精细的训练优化并不一致地提升迁移效果。

Conclusion: 贡献是一个可处理的优化模型和可审计的保留策略基准；实验度量的是可用前缀块与存储时间，而非 GPU 延迟。

Abstract: Retaining language-model prefix states trades recomputation against storage time. Optimizing each cached block independently can overcount savings: a resident block is usable only when the required preceding prefix is also available. We introduce Prefix-Certificate Retention (PCR), an exact finite-trace formulation for static, grouped, reset-on-access timeouts. Usable-prefix rewards become nodes whose prerequisites are timeout thresholds and preceding hit certificates. The resulting maximum-weight closure reduces to one minimum cut, with graph size linear in the number of block lookups and timeout choices. A breakpoint theorem extends the construction to all nonnegative timeouts without discretization error. We also derive a linear-time-in-grid-size dynamic program for ordered timeouts and bounds that certify the cost of this restriction. Exhaustive small-instance checks and chronological replay of 39,632 public Mooncake requests validate the formulation. On the fixed grid, ordered timeouts attain the unrestricted training optimum in 118 of 120 trace-grouping-price cases. Heterogeneous retention improves several held-out memory-time tradeoffs, but finer training optimization does not uniformly improve transfer. The contribution is a tractable optimization model and an auditable benchmark for retention policies; the experiments measure usable prefix blocks and storage time, not GPU latency.

</details>


### [132] [No-Free-Graph: Learning When Multimodal Data Should Be Graphified](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02768)
*Zekai Chen,Kai Hu,YuXin Zeng,Xunkai Li,Xun Wu,Yinlin Zhu,Zhengyu Wu,Xu Wang,Rong-Hua Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出MAG-SCOUT，一个"构图前"评估框架，先判断引入图结构是否有益，再决定是否构图，从而避免无效的图化开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态图学习多聚焦于如何构图与优化图，却很少追问一个更根本的问题：给定数据集与任务，是否真的应该引入额外的关系结构。作者通过跨数据集、任务和构图器的实证发现，图化并非总是有益——有时提升显著，有时收益有限甚至为负。因此主张把构图视为基于期望效用的选择性决策，而非默认的预处理步骤。

Method: 提出MAG-SCOUT，一种在生成完整拓扑之前的"预构图图评估"框架。它先采集有限的关系证据，分析其对具体任务的潜在贡献，进而估计图化的期望效用，并结合构造成本，做出"构建或跳过"（build-or-skip）的决策。

Result: 在六个多模态数据集、三个下游任务和多种图构造器上的大量实验表明，MAG-SCOUT能有效识别何时应当引入图结构：在预先设定的下限约束下，节省了33.1%的任务宏观图计算量，同时保留了96.7%的"留出正收益"质量。

Conclusion: 构图不应是默认步骤，而应是基于期望效用的选择性决策；MAG-SCOUT通过低成本、前置的效用评估，在保证大部分收益的同时显著减少无效图计算，为多模态图学习提供了更务实的实践范式。

Abstract: Multimodal graph learning has recently emerged as an effective paradigm for in corporating inter-entity relationships into multimodal representations. Existing studies have made substantial progress on how to construct and optimize graphs, but rarely consider a more fundamental question: whether additional relational structures should be introduced for a given dataset and task. Through empirical studies across diverse datasets, tasks, and graph constructors, we reveal that graphification is not consistently beneficial: introducing relational structures can provide substantial improvements in some cases, while offering limited or even negative gains. This observation motivates a new perspective that graph construction should be treated as a selective decision based on its expected utility rather than a default preprocessing step. To address this issue, we propose MAG-SCOUT, a pre-construction graph assessment framework that estimates whether introducing graph structures is beneficial before generating the complete topology. MAG-SCOUT collects limited relational evidence, analyzes its potential taskspecific contribution, and estimates the expected utility of graphification together with construction cost to make a build-or-skip decision. Extensive experiments across six multimodal datasets, three downstream tasks, and diverse graph constructors demonstrate that MAG-SCOUT effectively identifies when graph structures should be introduced, saving 33.1% of task-macro graph work while retaining 96.7% of held-out positive-gain mass under the pre-registered floor.

</details>


### [133] [Nearly Optimal Fixed-Confidence Best-Arm Identification with 1-Bit Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02771)
*Khang Luong,Dinh Thai Son,Hoang Ta,Hung The Tran,Tuan Quang Dam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究严格1比特反馈下的固定置信度最优臂识别问题，提出时间均匀的1比特均值估计原语，并嵌入到候选-挑战者算法中，给出PAC保证与间隙自适应样本复杂度，同时证明匹配的信息论下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在严格1比特反馈下，学习者每轮只能获得"采样奖励是否属于查询集"的单个比特，无法直接进行经验均值估计。现有方法依赖直接均值估计，在此设定下不再适用，因此需要新的估计原语与算法设计，并需要理解有限方差下1比特反馈带来的固有代价。

Method: 首先构造基于随机阈值查询与截断尾积分恒等式的时间均匀1比特均值估计原语；随后将该原语嵌入候选-挑战者式最优臂识别算法。提出固定截断算法（简单的anytime (ε,δ)-PAC保证）和分阶段自适应截断算法（将截断水平匹配当前分辨率，实现间隙自适应样本复杂度）。同时证明K臂最坏情况下的信息论下界。

Result: 固定截断算法给出简单的anytime (ε,δ)-PAC保证；分阶段自适应截断算法得到间隙自适应样本复杂度。下界证明有限方差1比特反馈导致的对数惩罚是固有的，该下界与分阶段算法的主导依赖项在低阶log log因子范围内相匹配。

Conclusion: 在严格1比特反馈与有限方差设定下，直接均值估计不可用，截断不可避免；本文提出的时间均匀1比特均值估计原语与自适应截断策略能够在候选-挑战者框架下实现间隙自适应的最优臂识别效率，且其性能与所证固有下界基本吻合，表明该对数惩罚无法被消除。

Abstract: We study fixed-confidence best-arm identification under strict 1-bit feedback constraints. At each round, the learner selects an arm and a query set, and receives only a single bit indicating whether the sampled reward belongs to that set. We consider a distribution-free finite-variance setting with arm-wise localization, where direct empirical mean estimation is no longer available and clipping becomes unavoidable. We first formulate a time-uniform 1-bit mean-estimation primitive based on randomized threshold queries and a clipped tail-integral identity. We then embed this primitive into candidate-challenger best-arm identification algorithms. A fixed-clipping algorithm gives a simple anytime $(ε,δ)$-PAC guarantee, while a phased adaptive-clipping algorithm matches the clipping level to the current resolution and yields a gap-adaptive sample complexity. We also prove a $K$-arm worst-case information-theoretic lower bound showing that the logarithmic penalty caused by finite-variance 1-bit feedback is intrinsic. This bound matches the leading dependence of the phased algorithm up to lower-order $\log\log$ factors.

</details>


### [134] [LatticeSMC: Where to Spend Inference-Time Compute in Chunked Sequence Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02774)
*Xuanchen Wang,Heng Wang,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出预算匹配的分块引导采样器 LatticeSMC，在块索引与去噪步的二维格上进行重采样，在同等算力下显著提升长序列生成的质量指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长序列生成（音乐、动作、视频）逐块生成、逐块去噪，而奖励定义在整条序列上；现有推理时引导方法只能沿单一轴操作（末端 best-of-N、跨去噪步的 Feynman-Kac、跨块流式剪枝），且比较时算力与回报规则不一致，缺乏统一、公平的对照。

Method: 提出预算匹配的分块引导框架，并给出 LatticeSMC 采样器：在块索引×去噪步的二维格上建立 Feynman-Kac 模型。两个 telescoping 结论保证设计精确：对块可加奖励，两轴诱导相同权重，故应在 lookahead 最便宜处重采样；对终端奖励，任意前缀分数可定义精确的中间势函数，使前缀可评估奖励成为无需估计、不增加去噪调用的 twist。据此在块边界按势重采样，打分免费时在块内也重采样，输出加权样本或最佳粒子。

Result: 在等算力条件下，于音乐到舞蹈扩散和 40 秒文本到音乐生成任务上，32 粒子时把节拍对齐从 0.234 提升到 0.441（best-of-N 为 0.354），提示遵循从 0.470 提升到 0.560，同时保持留出集质量；在长程奖励上仍具优势，人工评测中 60–77% 的成对比较更受偏好。

Conclusion: 承诺强度应跟随当前势函数所含信息，而 lookahead 的价值可由未来奖励的集合内可预测性来预测。

Abstract: Long-form generators for music, motion and video produce sequences chunk by chunk, with each chunk generated by iterative denoising while rewards are defined over the full sequence. Existing inference-time steering methods typically act on one axis at a time: best-of-N at the end, Feynman-Kac steering across denoising steps, or streaming pruning across chunks, and are often compared under unmatched compute or different return rules. We introduce budget-matched chunked steering and propose LatticeSMC, a sampler derived from a Feynman-Kac model on the two-dimensional lattice of chunk index and denoising step. Two telescoping results make its design exact: for chunk-additive rewards, the two axes induce identical weights, so resampling should occur where lookahead is cheapest; for terminal rewards, any prefix score defines an exact intermediate potential, making prefix-evaluable rewards twists with no estimation or extra denoiser calls. LatticeSMC resamples on these potentials at chunk boundaries and, when scoring is free, within chunks, returning either a weighted draw or the best particle. Under matched compute, on music-to-dance diffusion and 40-second text-to-music generation, it raises beat alignment from 0.234 to 0.441 (best-of-N: 0.354) and prompt adherence from 0.470 to 0.560 at 32 particles, while preserving held-out quality. It also retains its advantage on long-range rewards and is preferred by human raters in 60-77 percent of pairwise comparisons. Finally, we show that commitment strength should follow the information in the current potential, while the value of lookahead is predicted by the within-set predictability of future reward.

</details>


### [135] [Controlling Polar Exposure to Delay Memorization in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02780)
*Xuanchen Wang,Heng Wang,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Quality-Gated De-whitening (QGD)，通过保留快速极坐标更新前缀并逐步恢复固定增益动量，延缓样本记忆化，从而扩大扩散模型泛化窗口；配合 Copy-Budgeted Selection (CBS) 在 CIFAR-10 子集上使有效区间扩大 8.32x、复制减少 75.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在复制训练样本之前能达到可用的样本质量，但快速优化会加速样本特定拟合，压缩这一泛化窗口。作者希望从更新几何角度研究该效应，找到在保持质量到达时间的同时延缓记忆化的方法，以改善质量—复制的权衡。

Method: 提出 QGD 控制器：保留快速极坐标更新前缀，逐步恢复固定增益动量，包含确认质量门、有界衰减包络与因果复制反馈。随机特征分析区分协方差控制、曲率均衡与幅度控制三种记忆化时钟，并在对齐谱假设下给出有限曝光条件。另提出 CBS，对冻结检查点族进行同时二项校准，再对发布的检查点进行全新评估。

Result: 在 2,000 张图像的 CIFAR-10 子集上，QGD 保持极坐标基线的质量到达时间，同时将有效区间扩大 8.32x，并将常见检查点复制减少 75.9%。在相同校准与独立质量评估下，QGD 达到 FID 75.56，而相同选择器的 SGD 为 79.37。曝光匹配对照、独立检测器审计以及向流匹配和舞蹈生成的迁移均支持该方法。

Conclusion: 自适应曝光控制是改善质量—复制权衡的实用手段。QGD 通过延迟复制同时继续提升质量，为扩散模型在训练中保持泛化窗口提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models can reach useful sample quality before copying training examples, but fast optimization can compress this generalization window by accelerating sample-specific fitting. We investigate this effect through update geometry and propose Quality-Gated De-whitening (QGD), a controller that retains a fast polar-update prefix and progressively restores fixed-gain momentum. Our random-feature analysis separates covariance-controlled, curvature-equalized and amplitude-controlled memorization clocks. Under aligned spectral assumptions, it establishes a finite-exposure condition under which a fixed-gain tail recovers a delay proportional to dataset size. QGD implements this principle with a confirmed quality gate, a bounded decay envelope and causal copy feedback. Immediate switching is the conservative limit; gradual control balances delayed copying against continued quality improvement. We pair QGD with Copy-Budgeted Selection (CBS), which applies simultaneous binomial calibration to a frozen checkpoint family, followed by a fresh evaluation of the released checkpoint. On 2,000-image CIFAR-10 subsets, QGD preserves the polar baseline's quality-arrival time while expanding its useful interval by 8.32x and reducing common-checkpoint copying by 75.9%. With identical calibration and independent quality evaluation, QGD achieves FID 75.56 versus 79.37 for SGD with the same selector. Exposure-matched controls, independent detector audits and transfer to flow matching and dance generation support adaptive exposure control as a practical way to improve the quality-copying tradeoff.

</details>


### [136] [OPD Before RL: Warm-Starting Rubric-Based RL with On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02781)
*Xinpeng Wang,Wei Shi,Yu-Chia Chen,Maria Zontak,Yun He,Richard Yuanzhe Pang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两阶段训练框架：先用评分标准作为"特权教师上下文"进行密集的token级on-policy蒸馏（RP-OPD），再进行基于评分标准的强化学习（RL）。在HealthBench、ResearchQA和RubricHub Science上，该方法优于对照方法，且奖励黑客现象更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评分标准（rubric）RL能对开放式回答打分，但奖励在完整回答之后才给出，训练信号无法直接指出哪些具体决策影响了最终得分，导致监督稀疏、c额red它分配困难。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段RP-OPD，学生模型不接触评分标准，在自身生成的prefix上匹配一个"能看到评分标准"的教师的下一token分布，实现密集的token级监督；第二阶段用评分标准奖励做RL，在蒸馏平台上进一步提升。

Result: 在健康与科学类任务（HealthBench、ResearchQA、RubricHub Science）上，使用开放权重模型比较多种后训练方法，并变化RL前SFT或RP-OPD的训练量。两阶段框架在所有评估方法中得分最高；RP-OPD+RL在RubricHub Science上奖励黑客迹象有限，而SFT+RL基线越来越倾向于通过声称"符合评分标准"而非提供实质内容来获取高奖励。

Conclusion: 结果支持先用评分标准引导on-policy蒸馏，再应用基于评分标准的RL；该框架能更有效地利用评分标准信号并缓解奖励黑客问题。

Abstract: Many useful language-model tasks cannot be evaluated by exact outcome verification. Rubric-based reinforcement learning (RL) addresses this issue by scoring open-ended responses against explicit criteria. However, because the reward is assigned after the complete response, the training signal does not directly identify which individual decisions contributed to the final score. We propose a two-stage training framework that uses rubrics first as privileged teacher context for dense token-level supervision, then as rewards for further RL. In the first stage, rubric-privileged on-policy distillation (RP-OPD), a student without access to the rubric matches a rubric-aware teacher's next-token distributions at student-generated prefixes. In the second stage, RL directly optimizes the rubric reward and improves beyond the observed distillation plateau. We evaluate the framework on health and science tasks using open-weight models. Across HealthBench, ResearchQA, and RubricHub Science, we compare post-training methods and vary the amount of SFT or RP-OPD training before RL, finding that our two-stage framework achieves the highest scores among the methods evaluated. RP-OPD + RL shows limited signs of reward hacking on RubricHub Science, whereas the SFT + RL baseline increasingly receives high rewards for claims of rubric compliance without providing the required content. These findings support using rubrics to guide on-policy distillation before applying rubric-based RL.

</details>


### [137] [Efficient Memory Crystallization for Graph Learning under Non-Stationary Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02795)
*Yue Hou,Ruomei Liu,Yingke Su,Junran Wu,Ke Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Efficient Memory Crystallization (EMC)，一个无需训练的测试时框架，通过闭式解将每个到来的图域蒸馏为紧凑的记忆，实现高效的持续图适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实系统中的深度图学习模型常面临非平稳环境，图分布随时间持续漂移。现有解决方案依赖训练辅助生成模块来合成记忆图以进行跨域适应，计算开销大且在长期分布漂移下扩展性差。

Method: EMC 无需生成记忆，而是"结晶"记忆：通过面向记忆的分布匹配目标的闭式解，将每个到来的图域蒸馏为紧凑且语义忠实的记忆，消除持续协变量漂移下的冗余域信息；并通过状态演化记忆建模域间依赖关系。

Result: 在非平稳分布漂移的图上，EMC 优于最先进基线，相对最新竞品平均减少 87.4% 运行时间和 92.4% GPU 内存消耗。

Conclusion: EMC 是一个无需训练、高效且可扩展的持续图适应框架，具有更紧的泛化误差界，使大规模持续图适应变得实用。

Abstract: Deep graph learning models deployed in real-world systems often need to cope with non-stationary environments, where the underlying graph distribution drifts continually over time. Prevailing solutions rely on training auxiliary generative modules to synthesize memory graphs for cross-domain adaptation, which incurs substantial computational overhead and scales poorly under prolonged distribution shifts. We argue that a more economical path exists: rather than generating memory, one can crystallize it. To this end, we propose Efficient Memory Crystallization (EMC), a training-free test-time framework that distills each incoming graph domain into a compact, semantically faithful memory through a closed-form solution to a memory-oriented distribution-matching objective, thereby eliminating redundant domain information under continual covariate shifts. To preserve both generalizability and adaptability as the model traverses a long sequence of target domains, EMC further models inter-domain dependencies through state-evolving memories and admits a theoretically grounded, tighter generalization error bound than direct adaptation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superior performance of EMC over state-of-the-art baselines on graphs under non-stationary distribution shifts, while reducing average runtime by 87.4% and GPU memory consumption by 92.4% relative to the recent competitor, making continual graph adaptation practical at scale.

</details>


### [138] [Muon Learns Facts Better: Understanding the Role of Spectral Orthogonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02798)
*Xuheng Li,Qiwei Di,Yuan Cao,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过可处理的事实回忆模型，从机理上刻画了Muon（谱梯度流）相比普通梯度流与Sign梯度流在特征学习动力学上的差异，发现谱正交化能显著缩小主体与关系特征学习时间的差距，并改变误差衰减速率与嵌入等变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器对矩阵更新做谱正交化，在大规模训练中表现优异，但其对特征学习的作用机制尚不清楚，本文希望提供可解释的理论分析。

Method: 采用事实回忆模型：事实将"主体-关系"对映射到答案，线性Transformer学习主体依赖与关系依赖信息以恢复映射。分别用梯度流（GF）、谱梯度流（Spectral GF）、Sign GF（分别对应梯度下降、Muon、Adam的连续时间极限）进行优化，并用主体与关系分量达到目标精度所需的学习时间来刻画特征分离。

Result: 设主体数为S、关系数为R，GF的学习时间比为Õ(√(S/R))，而Spectral GF将其降至Õ(1)；固定S和R时，GF下主体与关系相关误差按1/(T log T)衰减，Spectral GF下按exp(-poly(T))衰减；此外GF与Spectral GF对token嵌入的正交变换等变，而Sign GF不等变，不同正交嵌入可能导致无特征分离、极大分离阶段甚至学习顺序反转。

Conclusion: 谱正交化从根本上重塑了特征学习动力学，本文为Muon类优化器的作用机理提供了理论解释。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer applies spectral orthogonalization to matrix-valued updates and has shown strong performance in large-scale neural network training, yet the mechanisms of this transformation in feature learning remain poorly understood. In this work, we investigate this question through a tractable factual-recall model, where a fact maps each subject-relation pair to an answer, and a linear transformer learns the subject- and relation-dependent information required to recover this mapping. The transformer is optimized with gradient flow (GF), spectral GF, or Sign GF, which are continuous-time limits of gradient descent, Muon, and Adam, respectively. Prior studies (Nichani et al., 2025) have shown that when the number of subjects exceeds the number of relations, GF learns relation-dependent information before subject-dependent information, producing a feature-separation phase during training. We characterize this separation with the learning times when the subject- and relation-dependent components of the prediction reach a target accuracy. With $S$ subjects and $R$ relations, GF has a learning-time ratio of $\widetildeΘ(\sqrt{S/R})$, whereas Spectral GF reduces this ratio to $\widetildeΘ(1)$. In addition, for fixed $S$ and $R$, the subject- and relation-dependent errors decay as $1/(T\log T)$ in training time $T$ under GF, but as $\exp(-\mathrm{poly}(T))$ under spectral GF. Finally, we show that GF and spectral GF are equivariant under orthogonal transformations of the token embeddings, whereas Sign GF is not: Different orthonormal embeddings can potentially produce no feature separation, a large feature-separation phase, or even a reversed learning order. These results provide a mechanistic view of how spectral orthogonalization can fundamentally reshape feature-learning dynamics.

</details>


### [139] [Gated Slot Attention-2: Two-Sided Associative Memory Correction in Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02816)
*Ruijie Li,Shengnan Ding,Weimin Zhang,Derick Tang,Zhanpeng Zeng,Qinsong Zeng,Ming Chen,Jiaxi Hu,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.LG
